[38] assistant: 按本地 PM skill `deliver-prd` + `develop-solution-brief` 的结构，产品方案如下。

**产品定位**
做一个 macOS + iOS 的类 Codex 交互应用，用户输入自己的 DeepSeek API Key 后，可以在本地与模型对话，并让模型围绕用户授权的工作区执行“代码理解、代码生成、修改建议、任务拆解”等操作。

核心不是做通用聊天机器人，而是做一个“本地工作区 AI 助手”。

**目标用户**
1. 独立开发者：想在 Mac/iPhone/iPad 上快速问代码、改代码、生成方案。
2. 产品/技术创作者：希望把想法转成 PRD、代码结构、原型任务。
3. 轻量团队成员：需要在移动端查看项目、继续和 AI 讨论代码任务。

**核心模块**
| 模块 | 作用 | P0/P1 |
|---|---|---|
| API Key 设置 | 输入、保存、检测 DeepSeek API Key | P0 |
| 聊天模块 | 类 Codex 对话流，支持上下文、多轮、模型选择 | P0 |
| 代码模块 | 选择 workspace，读取文件，生成/解释/修改代码建议 | P0 |
| 任务执行模块 | 把用户意图拆成步骤，生成待执行操作 | P1 |
| 历史记录 | 保存会话、任务、代码上下文 | P1 |
| 安全确认 | 写文件、运行命令前必须确认 | P0 |

**用户主流程**
1. 首次打开 App。
2. 用户输入 DeepSeek API Key。
3. App 校验 Key 是否可用。
4. 用户进入主界面，选择“聊天”或“代码”。
5. 聊天模式：直接对话、提问、写方案、分析需求。
6. 代码模式：选择本地 workspace，AI 读取文件上下文，回答代码问题或生成修改方案。
7. 如果涉及写文件、执行命令、调用本地任务，App 展示操作计划，用户确认后执行。

**信息架构**
主界面建议采用三栏结构：

| 区域 | macOS | iOS |
|---|---|---|
| 左侧 | 会话列表、workspace 列表、模式切换 | Tab / 抽屉 |
| 中间 | 聊天主窗口 | 主聊天页 |
| 右侧 | 当前任务、文件上下文、执行记录 | 二级详情页 |

两个一级入口：

1. **Chat**
   - 普通对话
   - 产品方案
   - prompt 调试
   - 多轮上下文

2. **Code**
   - 选择项目目录
   - 文件索引
   - 代码解释
   - 代码生成
   - diff 预览
   - 用户确认后写入

**系统架构**
```text
SwiftUI App
  ├─ Key Management
  │   └─ Keychain 保存 DeepSeek API Key
  ├─ Chat Engine
  │   └─ DeepSeek API Adapter
  ├─ Code Workspace Engine
  │   ├─ 文件选择
  │   ├─ 文件读取
  │   ├─ 上下文裁剪
  │   └─ diff 生成
  ├─ Task Executor
  │   ├─ 任务计划
  │   ├─ 用户确认
  │   └─ 本地执行/写文件
  └─ Conversation Store
      └─ 本地会话与任务历史
```

**DeepSeek API 设计**
产品层只暴露“模型服务”，不要把 DeepSeek 调用散落到 UI 里。

建议抽象为：

```text
ModelProvider
  ├─ sendChat(messages)
  ├─ streamChat(messages)
  ├─ summarizeFiles(files)
  └─ proposeCodeChange(task, context)
```

这样后续可以扩展 OpenAI、Claude、本地模型，但 MVP 只接 DeepSeek。

**安全边界**
1. API Key 只保存在本机 Keychain。
2. 默认不上传整个 workspace，只上传用户当前选择的文件或必要摘要。
3. 写文件前展示 diff。
4. 执行命令前展示命令、目录、风险说明。
5. iOS 端默认以“查看、讨论、生成方案”为主；macOS 端承担完整 workspace 执行能力。

**MVP 范围**
P0 先做：

1. macOS/iOS SwiftUI 通用 App。
2. DeepSeek API Key 输入、保存、校验。
3. 聊天界面，支持流式返回。
4. Code 模式：选择文件、读取内容、让模型解释/生成修改建议。
5. 写入前 diff 预览和确认。

暂不做：

1. 自动全项目重构。
2. 自动执行危险 shell 命令。
3. 多模型 marketplace。
4. 团队协作和云同步。
5. 插件系统。

**成功标准**
| 指标 | MVP 目标 |
|---|---|
| 首次配置成功率 | 90% 用户 1 分钟内完成 API Key 配置 |
| 首次有效对话 | 配置后 10 秒内收到模型回复 |
| 代码任务完成率 | 用户能完成“解释文件 / 生成修改建议 / 应用 diff”三类任务 |
| 安全误操作 | 写文件和执行命令均需要确认，无静默修改 |

**建议产品名**
可以先叫：`DeepCodex`、`KeyCode Agent`、`Local Coding Agent`。如果偏中文，可以叫 `本地代码助手` 或 `DeepSeek Codex`。

[45] assistant: **优化后的产品定位**
这款 App 不再只是“类 Codex 聊天工具”，而是一个面向儿童的 AI 创作学习工作台。核心能力是：孩子用自然语言提出想法，App 通过 DeepSeek API 调用模型，再把本地 Skill 能力包装成可点击、可引导、可确认的创作工具。

主定位：

> 一个儿童友好的 macOS / iOS AI 创作助手，支持聊天、代码学习、PPT 制作、网页制作，并通过提示卡片引导孩子安全地表达需求。

**核心能力模块**
| 模块 | 说明 | 优先级 |
|---|---|---|
| AI 聊天 | 儿童友好的问答、解释、学习陪伴 | P0 |
| 代码助手 | 解释代码、生成小程序、学习编程概念 | P0 |
| PPT 制作 Skill | 将主题转成大纲、页面结构、讲稿、PPT 文件 | P0 |
| 网页制作 Skill | 将想法转成儿童可预览的网页、小作品、互动页面 | P0 |
| 提示能力 | 用提示卡、模板、分步问题帮助孩子说清楚需求 | P0 |
| 家长/老师确认 | API Key、文件写入、网页导出、代码执行前确认 | P0 |
| 作品库 | 保存孩子做过的 PPT、网页、代码作品 | P1 |

**产品信息架构**
```text
儿童 AI 创作 App
  ├─ 聊天
  │   ├─ 问问题
  │   ├─ 学知识
  │   └─ 解释概念
  ├─ 做 PPT
  │   ├─ 选主题
  │   ├─ 生成大纲
  │   ├─ 生成页面
  │   └─ 导出 PPT
  ├─ 做网页
  │   ├─ 选主题
  │   ├─ 选风格
  │   ├─ 生成页面
  │   └─ 本地预览/导出
  ├─ 学代码
  │   ├─ 解释代码
  │   ├─ 生成小游戏/小工具
  │   └─ 分步教学
  └─ 作品库
      ├─ 我的 PPT
      ├─ 我的网页
      └─ 我的代码
```

**Skill 打包方式**
不要让孩子直接看到“技能文件、prompt、工具链”。App 应该把 Skill 包装成能力卡片：

1. **PPT 能力卡**
   - “做一个关于恐龙的 5 页 PPT”
   - “帮我做英语课演讲”
   - “把我的想法变成演示稿”
   - 背后调用：`presentations-premium` 或 `utility-slideshow-creator`

2. **网页能力卡**
   - “做一个我的个人主页”
   - “做一个动物百科网页”
   - “做一个数学小游戏页面”
   - 背后调用：`frontend-app-builder-pro`、`frontend-testing-debugging-pro`

3. **代码能力卡**
   - “解释这段代码”
   - “帮我做一个小游戏”
   - “一步一步教我改代码”

4. **聊天能力卡**
   - “像老师一样解释”
   - “帮我想创意”
   - “把难的内容讲简单”

**推荐系统架构**
```text
SwiftUI App
  ├─ Child Home UI
  │   ├─ Chat
  │   ├─ Make Slides
  │   ├─ Make Website
  │   ├─ Learn Code
  │   └─ My Works
  │
  ├─ Prompt Coach
  │   ├─ 提示卡模板
  │   ├─ 年龄适配
  │   ├─ 分步追问
  │   └─ 安全改写
  │
  ├─ Skill Hub
  │   ├─ PPT Skill Adapter
  │   ├─ Web Skill Adapter
  │   ├─ Code Skill Adapter
  │   └─ Chat Skill Adapter
  │
  ├─ Model Gateway
  │   └─ DeepSeek API
  │
  ├─ Safety Layer
  │   ├─ 家长 API Key 管理
  │   ├─ 内容安全过滤
  │   ├─ 文件写入确认
  │   └─ 执行命令确认
  │
  └─ Local Storage
      ├─ Keychain
      ├─ 会话记录
      ├─ 作品文件
      └─ Skill 配置
```

**儿童提示能力设计**
儿童通常不会写完整 prompt，所以 App 需要把 prompt 变成“可选择的问题”。

例如做 PPT：

```text
你想做什么主题？
[恐龙] [太空] [海洋] [我的宠物] [自己输入]

你想做几页？
[3 页] [5 页] [8 页]

你想给谁看？
[老师] [同学] [爸爸妈妈]

你想要什么风格？
[可爱] [科学] [故事] [彩色]
```

最终由 App 自动生成完整 prompt：

```text
请为 8 岁儿童生成一个 5 页 PPT，主题是太空。
语言简单、有趣，每页有标题、3 个要点和一段演讲稿。
风格活泼，适合课堂展示。
```

**儿童安全边界**
1. API Key 由家长或老师输入，孩子不可直接查看。
2. 默认不联网搜索，除非家长开启。
3. 默认不执行本地命令。
4. 写文件、导出网页、生成代码项目前必须确认。
5. 对儿童输入做安全改写，避免暴力、成人、隐私、危险实验等内容。
6. 不鼓励孩子输入真实姓名、学校、住址、电话。
7. 所有作品默认保存在本地。
8. iOS 端以创作和查看为主，macOS 端支持更完整的本地文件和 Skill 执行。

**MVP 建议**
第一版只做四个入口：

1. **问一问**
   - DeepSeek 聊天
   - 儿童语气
   - 可选择“讲简单一点 / 举例子 / 出题考我”

2. **做 PPT**
   - 主题输入
   - 自动生成大纲
   - 自动生成 slides 文案
   - 导出 PPT 或先生成 Markdown/HTML 预览

3. **做网页**
   - 主题输入
   - 选择页面类型
   - 生成单页网页
   - 本地预览

4. **学代码**
   - 解释代码
   - 生成简单 HTML/JS 小作品
   - 每一步都用儿童能理解的话解释

**产品原则**
这款 App 的重点不是“给孩子一个强大的 AI 控制台”，而是“把强大的能力变成安全、清楚、可学习的创作按钮”。

因此界面上不要出现太多技术词，比如 API、Skill、Model、Workspace。可以替换成：

| 技术词 | 儿童界面文案 |
|---|---|
| Skill | 能力 |
| Prompt | 告诉 AI 你的想法 |
| Workspace | 我的作品文件夹 |
| Execute | 开始制作 |
| Diff | 看看改了什么 |
| Export | 保存作品 |
| Model | AI 大脑 |

**一句话版本**
这是一个儿童 AI 创作工作台：家长配置 DeepSeek API Key，孩子通过提示卡片调用聊天、PPT、网页和代码能力，在安全确认机制下完成自己的学习和创作作品。

---
name: swiftpm-macos
description: Build, run, and test pure SwiftPM-based macOS packages and executables. Use when the repo is package-first, when there is no Xcode project, or when Swift package workflows are the fastest path to diagnosis.
---

# SwiftPM for macOS

## Quick Start

Use this skill when `Package.swift` is the primary entrypoint or when SwiftPM is
the fastest path to a reproducible result.

## Workflow

1. Inspect the package.
   - Read `Package.swift`.
   - Identify executable, library, and test products.

2. Build with SwiftPM.
   - Use `swift build` by default.
   - Use release mode only when the user explicitly needs it.

3. Run the right product.
   - Use `swift run <product>` when an executable exists.
   - If multiple executables exist, explain the default choice.

4. Test narrowly.
   - Use `swift test`.
   - Apply filters when a specific test target or case is known.

5. Summarize failures.
   - Module/import resolution
   - Package graph or dependency issue
   - Linker failure
   - Runtime failure
   - Test regression

## Guardrails

- Prefer SwiftPM over Xcode when both exist and the package path is clearly simpler.
- Do not assume an app bundle exists in a pure package workflow.
- Explain when the package is library-only and therefore not directly runnable.

## Output Expectations

Provide:
- the package products you found
- the command you ran
- whether build, run, or test succeeded
- the top blocker if not

name: build-run-debug
description: Build, run, and debug local macOS apps and desktop executables using shell-first Xcode and Swift workflows. Use when asked to build a Mac app, launch it, diagnose compiler or linker failures, inspect startup problems, or debug desktop-only runtime issues.
---

# Build / Run / Debug

## Quick Start

Use this skill to set up one project-local `script/build_and_run.sh` entrypoint,
wire `.codex/environments/environment.toml` so the Codex app shows a Run button,
then use that script as the default build/run path.

Prefer shell-first workflows:

- `./script/build_and_run.sh` as the single kill + build + run entrypoint once it exists
- `xcodebuild` for Xcode workspaces or projects
- `swift build` plus raw executable launch inside that script for true SwiftPM command-line tools
- `swift build` plus project-local `.app` bundle staging and `/usr/bin/open -n` launch for SwiftPM AppKit/SwiftUI GUI apps
- optional script flags for `lldb`, `log stream`, telemetry verification, or post-launch process checks

Do not assume simulators, touch interaction, or mobile-specific tooling.

If an Xcode-aware MCP surface is already available and the user explicitly wants
it, use it only where it fits. Keep that usage narrow and honest: prefer it for
Xcode-oriented discovery, logging, or debugging support, and do not force
simulator-specific workflows onto pure macOS tasks.

## Workflow

1. Discover the project shape.
   - Check whether the workspace is already inside a git repo with `git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree`.
   - If no git repo is present, run `git init` at the project/workspace root before building so Codex app git-backed features are available. Never run `git init` inside a nested subdirectory when the current workspace already belongs to a parent repo.
   - Look for `.xcworkspace`, `.xcodeproj`, and `Package.swift<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1036" />y available, prefer it over ad hoc setup.
   - Use the MCP for Xcode-aware discovery or debug/logging workflows when the available tool surface clearly matches the task.
   - Fall back to shell commands immediately when the MCP does not provide a clean macOS path.

## Preferred Commands

- Project discovery:
  - `find . -name '*.xcworkspace' -o -name '*.xcodeproj' -o -name 'Package.swift'`
- Scheme discovery:
  - `xcodebuild -list -workspace <workspace>`
  - `xcodebuild -list -project <project>`
- Build/run:
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --debug`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --logs`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --telemetry`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --verify`

## References

- `references/run-button-bootstrap.md`: canonical `build_and_run.sh` and `.codex/environments/environment.toml` contract.

## Guardrails

- Prefer the narrowest command that proves or disproves the current theory.
- Do not leave the user with a one-off manual command chain once a stable `build_and_run.sh` script can own the workflow.
- Do not write `.codex/environments/environment.toml` before the run script exists, and do not point the Run action at a stale script path.
- Do not launch a SwiftUI/AppKit SwiftPM GUI app as a raw executable unless the user explicitly wants to diagnose that failure mode: it can produce no Dock icon, no foreground activation, and missing bundle identifier warnings. Keep raw executable launch only for true command-line tools.
- Do not claim UI state you cannot inspect directly.
- Do not describe mobile or simulator workflows as if they apply to macOS.
- If build output is huge, summarize the first real blocker and point to follow-up commands.

## Output Expectations

Provide:
- the detected project type
- the script path and Codex environment action you configured, if applicable
- the command you ran
- whether build and launch succeeded
- the top blocker if they failed
- the smallest sensible next action

---
name: Presentations
description: Build premium editorial analytics PPTX decks with artifact-tool presentation JSX, using ruthless narrative editing, chart-first storytelling, rendered critique, and iteration until the output beats the reference deck.
---

# Presentations

`Presentations` is for serious, high-polish presentation work where "clean" is
not enough. The target is an editable PowerPoint deck that feels like a strong
editor, a strong analyst, and a strong designer built it together.

Use this skill for analytics narratives, investor/operating reviews, strategy
stories, product/business performance decks, and any PPTX task where the user
asks to beat a reference deck.

## Operating Contract

Use artifact-tool presentation JSX only. The bundled Codex runtime provides
`@oai/artifact-tool` version 2.7.3 or newer and exposes
`@oai/artifact-tool/presentation-jsx`.

Do not require or import a separate presentation runtime package. PPTX
export/render behavior must be accessed through artifact-tool.

For generated raster images or reference comps, use the Codex imagegen tool.
Bundled scripts may write imagegen prompt files, but must not call external
image APIs or read API keys.

Thread-scoped paths:

- `SKILL_DIR=<absolute path to the installed Presentations skill>`
- `THREAD_ID=${CODEX_THREAD_ID:-manual-<timestamp-or-short-random-suffix>}`
- `WORKSPACE=$PWD/outputs/$THREAD_ID/presentations/<task-slug>`
- `SLIDES_DIR=$WORKSPACE/slides`
- `PREVIEW_DIR=$WORKSPACE/preview`
- `LAYOUT_DIR=$WORKSPACE/layout`
- `ASSET_DIR=$WORKSPACE/assets`
- `QA_DIR=$WORKSPACE/qa`
- `OUTPUT_DIR=<user-provided output dir if any, otherwise $WORKSPACE/output>`
- `FINAL_PPTX=$OUTPUT_DIR/<relevant-deck-title-slug>.pptx`

Use absolute paths in commands and handoffs. Keep all generated planning text
notes, preview PNGs, contact sheets, layout JSON, imagegen prompt f<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="421" />rt the final PPTX only after the rendered deck beats the target or the
    remaining gap is explicitly documented.
11. Clean up generated planning text notes, QA scratch files, contact sheets,
    preview images, generated slide modules/scripts, layout JSON, manifests,
    prompt text files, and other temporary images before the final response.

### Task Modes

- `reference-beating`: user supplied or named a deck to beat. This is the
  default when a reference PPTX is present.
- `template-following`: user supplied a source/template deck whose slide
  skeletons and visual system must be inherited.
- `create`: no deck/template is supplied; build from prompt and sources.
- `targeted-edit`: small changes to an existing deck.

When both a source deck and a better reference deck exist, separate them:

- source deck: content, required sections, facts, existing material.
- reference deck: quality bar, rhythm, taste, and proof that a stronger output
  is possible.

Do not blindly clone the reference. Beat it by improving story precision,
composition variety, chart clarity, whitespace, and final render quality.

### Deck Profile Router

After task mode, choose exactly one primary `deck-profile`. This is a hard
routing step, not a labeling exercise. The profile determines which proof
objects, source rules, visual density, asset rules, and QA gates are blocking.

Profiles:

- `finance-ir`: earnings, investor relations, operating reviews, financial
  analysis. Requires exact reported figures, unit discipline, source footnotes,
  bridges, tables, and disclosure logic.
- `product-platform`: SaaS/platform/product narratives. Requires architecture
  maps, workflow diagrams, adoption proof, product-to-financial linkage, and
  no generic feature-card grids.
- `gtm-growth`: GTM, marketing, consumer growth, subscription ecosystems,
  mobility, customer engagement. Requires a visible growth loop, segment or
  cohort proof, monetization bridge, and brand-aware rhythm.

name: utility-slideshow-creator
description: Generates professional presentations from a JSON deck specification using 18 slide types with dark/light variants, content-to-layout decision logic, and calibrated character limits. Ships with a default professional theme and supports custom themes via utility-slideshow-themer.

<!-- PM-Skills | https://github.com/product-on-purpose/pm-skills | Apache 2.0 -->

# Slideshow Creator

Generate professional presentations (.pptx and .pdf) from a JSON deck specification. Zero design decisions at generation time . Claude selects slide types and fills content slots; all visual properties are pre-decided by the theme.

## When to Use

- Creating slide decks for stakeholder updates, product reviews, team presentations
- Generating professional .pptx files from content briefs
- Producing consistent presentations without manual formatting
- Exporting presentations to PDF for sharing or archiving

## When NOT to Use

- Creating complex data visualizations (use dedicated charting tools)
- Building interactive web presentations (this produces .pptx, not HTML)
- Editing existing PowerPoint files (this creates new decks from scratch)

## How It Works

**Two-phase architecture:**

1. **Spec phase (Claude)** . Read the content brief, select slide types using decision logic, write a JSON deck specification. Content slots have character limits calibrated to prevent overflow. This is the only phase that costs tokens.
2. **Generation phase (Local script)** . Run `node scripts/generate-deck.js deck-spec.json` to produce .pptx. Optionally run `node scripts/export-pdf.mjs deck-spec.json` for PDF. Deterministic rendering, zero token cost.

Both <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="336" />ng between topics | `section` |
| Paragraph explanation | `content` |
| List of 3-6 points | `bullets` |
| Side-by-side comparison | `two_col` |
| Single key metric | `stat` |
| Two metrics compared | `dual_stat` |
| Testimonial or pull quote | `quote` |
| Three parallel concepts | `three_card` |
| Four concepts in a grid | `four_grid` |
| Dates or milestones | `timeline` |
| Sequential workflow | `process_flow` |
| Meeting agenda | `agenda` |
| Key finding or summary | `highlight` |
| Tabular data | `table` |
| Feature list with markers | `icon_rows` |
| Ending the deck | `closing` |

Full decision logic with variant strategy: `references/decision-logic.md`

## JSON Deck Spec Format

```json
{
  "title": "Q3 Product Update",
  "author": "Product Team",
  "footerText": "Internal . Q3 Review",
  "slides": [
    { "type": "title_dark", "title": "Q3 Product Update", "subtitle": "October 2026" },
    { "type": "stat", "stat": "94%", "label": "Customer satisfaction score", "accentColor": "secondary" },
    { "type": "bullets", "title": "What Shipped", "bullets": ["Feature A", "Feature B", "Feature C"] },
    { "type": "closing" }
  ]
}
```

Colors accept theme token names (`"accent"`, `"secondary"`, `"tertiary"`, `"warm"`) or 6-character hex strings (`"2563EB"`).

Full schema and workflow: `references/TEMPLATE.md` and `references/platform-rules.md`

## Instructions

1. **Read the content brief** . Understand topic, audience, length, specific requirements
2. **Plan the deck** . Select slide types using the decision logic table. Assign dark/light variants for visual rhythm (alternate to avoid monotony).
3. **Write the JSON deck specification** . Fill content slots, respecting character limits from `references/slide-types.md`
4. **Run the generation script** . `node scripts/generate-deck.js deck-spec.json`
5. **Optionally export PDF** . `node scripts/export-pdf.mjs deck-spec.json output.pdf`
6. **Report the output** . Tell the user where the file(s) are

name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.
---

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2040" />eliberately. Do not rely on browser defaults or inherited `16px` sizing for buttons, tabs, inputs, toolbars, sidebars, inspector panels, layer rows, status bars, command palettes, or export/share controls.
- Preserve the container model. Do not add cards, bordered panels, floating containers, tiles, or card grids where the spec uses open whitespace, bands, rails, lists, tables, canvases, or full-bleed composition.
- Keep real interactive app UI text, navigation, buttons, forms, tables, controls, and labels code-native. This does not apply to text and branding that belong inside product images, posters, packaging, signs, background scenes, hero photos, or other raster assets. Do not ship a static screenshot as UI.
- Use Image Gen for central non-icon assets. Render product images and background assets completely with the needed text, logos, marks, labels, packaging, signage, and branding. When the asset must layer into the UI, request a transparent background or clean cutout. Quote exact asset text and require verbatim rendering when text matters.
- If the accepted design includes branded product imagery, use Image Gen editing to create standalone versions of the logo/product/packaging/signage assets from the concept or a matching asset pass. Include transparent-background variants when those assets need to layer into the UI. Do not rebuild branded raster assets from generic CSS, mismatched fonts, or approximate labels.
- For games, use Image Gen for visible production art: character/state sprites or sprite sheets, terrain/platform tiles, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, foreground props, and parallax/background layers. Do not fall back to canvas-drawn shapes because collision, scaling, or animation is simpler. Keep HUD text, score, controls, hit boxes, physics, and game state code-native, and tune collision geometry to the rendered assets. Any code-drawn or vector game art must be listed as an intentional deviation or concrete blocker.

+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
+PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
+DIST="$ROOT/dist"
+BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
+EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"
+
+cd "$ROOT"
+
+pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
+
+swift build
+
+rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
+mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
+cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"
+
+cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>

+<plist version="1.0">

+  <string>$APP_NAME</string>

+  <key>CFBundleName</key>

+  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
+  <string>NSApplication</string>
+</dict>
+</plist>
+PLIST
+
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
+  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
+  sleep 2
+  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
+  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
+else
+  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
+fi

[38] assistant: 按本地 PM skill `deliver-prd` + `develop-solution-brief` 的结构，产品方案如下。

**产品定位**
做一个 macOS + iOS 的类 Codex 交互应用，用户输入自己的 DeepSeek API Key 后，可以在本地与模型对话，并让模型围绕用户授权的工作区执行“代码理解、代码生成、修改建议、任务拆解”等操作。

核心不是做通用聊天机器人，而是做一个“本地工作区 AI 助手”。

**目标用户**
1. 独立开发者：想在 Mac/iPhone/iPad 上快速问代码、改代码、生成方案。
2. 产品/技术创作者：希望把想法转成 PRD、代码结构、原型任务。
3. 轻量团队成员：需要在移动端查看项目、继续和 AI 讨论代码任务。

**核心模块**
| 模块 | 作用 | P0/P1 |
|---|---|---|
| API Key 设置 | 输入、保存、检测 DeepSeek API Key | P0 |
| 聊天模块 | 类 Codex 对话流，支持上下文、多轮、模型选择 | P0 |
| 代码模块 | 选择 workspace，读取文件，生成/解释/修改代码建议 | P0 |
| 任务执行模块 | 把用户意图拆成步骤，生成待执行操作 | P1 |
| 历史记录 | 保存会话、任务、代码上下文 | P1 |
| 安全确认 | 写文件、运行命令前必须确认 | P0 |

**用户主流程**
1. 首次打开 App。
2. 用户输入 DeepSeek API Key。
3. App 校验 Key 是否可用。
4. 用户进入主界面，选择“聊天”或“代码”。
5. 聊天模式：直接对话、提问、写方案、分析需求。
6. 代码模式：选择本地 workspace，AI 读取文件上下文，回答代码问题或生成修改方案。
7. 如果涉及写文件、执行命令、调用本地任务，App 展示操作计划，用户确认后执行。

**信息架构**
主界面建议采用三栏结构：

| 区域 | macOS | iOS |
|---|---|---|
| 左侧 | 会话列表、workspace 列表、模式切换 | Tab / 抽屉 |
| 中间 | 聊天主窗口 | 主聊天页 |
| 右侧 | 当前任务、文件上下文、执行记录 | 二级详情页 |

两个一级入口：

1. **Chat**
   - 普通对话
   - 产品方案
   - prompt 调试
   - 多轮上下文

2. **Code**
   - 选择项目目录
   - 文件索引
   - 代码解释
   - 代码生成
   - diff 预览
   - 用户确认后写入

**系统架构**
```text
SwiftUI App
  ├─ Key Management
  │   └─ Keychain 保存 DeepSeek API Key
  ├─ Chat Engine
  │   └─ DeepSeek API Adapter
  ├─ Code Workspace Engine
  │   ├─ 文件选择
  │   ├─ 文件读取
  │   ├─ 上下文裁剪
  │   └─ diff 生成
  ├─ Task Executor
  │   ├─ 任务计划
  │   ├─ 用户确认
  │   └─ 本地执行/写文件
  └─ Conversation Store
      └─ 本地会话与任务历史
```

**DeepSeek API 设计**
产品层只暴露“模型服务”，不要把 DeepSeek 调用散落到 UI 里。

建议抽象为：

```text
ModelProvider
  ├─ sendChat(messages)
  ├─ streamChat(messages)
  ├─ summarizeFiles(files)
  └─ proposeCodeChange(task, context)
```

这样后续可以扩展 OpenAI、Claude、本地模型，但 MVP 只接 DeepSeek。

**安全边界**
1. API Key 只保存在本机 Keychain。
2. 默认不上传整个 workspace，只上传用户当前选择的文件或必要摘要。
3. 写文件前展示 diff。
4. 执行命令前展示命令、目录、风险说明。
5. iOS 端默认以“查看、讨论、生成方案”为主；macOS 端承担完整 workspace 执行能力。

**MVP 范围**
P0 先做：

1. macOS/iOS SwiftUI 通用 App。
2. DeepSeek API Key 输入、保存、校验。
3. 聊天界面，支持流式返回。
4. Code 模式：选择文件、读取内容、让模型解释/生成修改建议。
5. 写入前 diff 预览和确认。

暂不做：

1. 自动全项目重构。
2. 自动执行危险 shell 命令。
3. 多模型 marketplace。
4. 团队协作和云同步。
5. 插件系统。

**成功标准**
| 指标 | MVP 目标 |
|---|---|
| 首次配置成功率 | 90% 用户 1 分钟内完成 API Key 配置 |
| 首次有效对话 | 配置后 10 秒内收到模型回复 |
| 代码任务完成率 | 用户能完成“解释文件 / 生成修改建议 / 应用 diff”三类任务 |
| 安全误操作 | 写文件和执行命令均需要确认，无静默修改 |

**建议产品名**
可以先叫：`DeepCodex`、`KeyCode Agent`、`Local Coding Agent`。如果偏中文，可以叫 `本地代码助手` 或 `DeepSeek Codex`。

[45] assistant: **优化后的产品定位**
这款 App 不再只是“类 Codex 聊天工具”，而是一个面向儿童的 AI 创作学习工作台。核心能力是：孩子用自然语言提出想法，App 通过 DeepSeek API 调用模型，再把本地 Skill 能力包装成可点击、可引导、可确认的创作工具。

主定位：

> 一个儿童友好的 macOS / iOS AI 创作助手，支持聊天、代码学习、PPT 制作、网页制作，并通过提示卡片引导孩子安全地表达需求。

**核心能力模块**
| 模块 | 说明 | 优先级 |
|---|---|---|
| AI 聊天 | 儿童友好的问答、解释、学习陪伴 | P0 |
| 代码助手 | 解释代码、生成小程序、学习编程概念 | P0 |
| PPT 制作 Skill | 将主题转成大纲、页面结构、讲稿、PPT 文件 | P0 |
| 网页制作 Skill | 将想法转成儿童可预览的网页、小作品、互动页面 | P0 |
| 提示能力 | 用提示卡、模板、分步问题帮助孩子说清楚需求 | P0 |
| 家长/老师确认 | API Key、文件写入、网页导出、代码执行前确认 | P0 |
| 作品库 | 保存孩子做过的 PPT、网页、代码作品 | P1 |

**产品信息架构**
```text
儿童 AI 创作 App
  ├─ 聊天
  │   ├─ 问问题
  │   ├─ 学知识
  │   └─ 解释概念
  ├─ 做 PPT
  │   ├─ 选主题
  │   ├─ 生成大纲
  │   ├─ 生成页面
  │   └─ 导出 PPT
  ├─ 做网页
  │   ├─ 选主题
  │   ├─ 选风格
  │   ├─ 生成页面
  │   └─ 本地预览/导出
  ├─ 学代码
  │   ├─ 解释代码
  │   ├─ 生成小游戏/小工具
  │   └─ 分步教学
  └─ 作品库
      ├─ 我的 PPT
      ├─ 我的网页
      └─ 我的代码
```

**Skill 打包方式**
不要让孩子直接看到“技能文件、prompt、工具链”。App 应该把 Skill 包装成能力卡片：

1. **PPT 能力卡**
   - “做一个关于恐龙的 5 页 PPT”
   - “帮我做英语课演讲”
   - “把我的想法变成演示稿”
   - 背后调用：`presentations-premium` 或 `utility-slideshow-creator`

2. **网页能力卡**
   - “做一个我的个人主页”
   - “做一个动物百科网页”
   - “做一个数学小游戏页面”
   - 背后调用：`frontend-app-builder-pro`、`frontend-testing-debugging-pro`

3. **代码能力卡**
   - “解释这段代码”
   - “帮我做一个小游戏”
   - “一步一步教我改代码”

4. **聊天能力卡**
   - “像老师一样解释”
   - “帮我想创意”
   - “把难的内容讲简单”

**推荐系统架构**
```text
SwiftUI App
  ├─ Child Home UI
  │   ├─ Chat
  │   ├─ Make Slides
  │   ├─ Make Website
  │   ├─ Learn Code
  │   └─ My Works
  │
  ├─ Prompt Coach
  │   ├─ 提示卡模板
  │   ├─ 年龄适配
  │   ├─ 分步追问
  │   └─ 安全改写
  │
  ├─ Skill Hub
  │   ├─ PPT Skill Adapter
  │   ├─ Web Skill Adapter
  │   ├─ Code Skill Adapter
  │   └─ Chat Skill Adapter
  │
  ├─ Model Gateway
  │   └─ DeepSeek API
  │
  ├─ Safety Layer
  │   ├─ 家长 API Key 管理
  │   ├─ 内容安全过滤
  │   ├─ 文件写入确认
  │   └─ 执行命令确认
  │
  └─ Local Storage
      ├─ Keychain
      ├─ 会话记录
      ├─ 作品文件
      └─ Skill 配置
```

**儿童提示能力设计**
儿童通常不会写完整 prompt，所以 App 需要把 prompt 变成“可选择的问题”。

例如做 PPT：

```text
你想做什么主题？
[恐龙] [太空] [海洋] [我的宠物] [自己输入]

你想做几页？
[3 页] [5 页] [8 页]

你想给谁看？
[老师] [同学] [爸爸妈妈]

你想要什么风格？
[可爱] [科学] [故事] [彩色]
```

最终由 App 自动生成完整 prompt：

```text
请为 8 岁儿童生成一个 5 页 PPT，主题是太空。
语言简单、有趣，每页有标题、3 个要点和一段演讲稿。
风格活泼，适合课堂展示。
```

**儿童安全边界**
1. API Key 由家长或老师输入，孩子不可直接查看。
2. 默认不联网搜索，除非家长开启。
3. 默认不执行本地命令。
4. 写文件、导出网页、生成代码项目前必须确认。
5. 对儿童输入做安全改写，避免暴力、成人、隐私、危险实验等内容。
6. 不鼓励孩子输入真实姓名、学校、住址、电话。
7. 所有作品默认保存在本地。
8. iOS 端以创作和查看为主，macOS 端支持更完整的本地文件和 Skill 执行。

**MVP 建议**
第一版只做四个入口：

1. **问一问**
   - DeepSeek 聊天
   - 儿童语气
   - 可选择“讲简单一点 / 举例子 / 出题考我”

2. **做 PPT**
   - 主题输入
   - 自动生成大纲
   - 自动生成 slides 文案
   - 导出 PPT 或先生成 Markdown/HTML 预览

3. **做网页**
   - 主题输入
   - 选择页面类型
   - 生成单页网页
   - 本地预览

4. **学代码**
   - 解释代码
   - 生成简单 HTML/JS 小作品
   - 每一步都用儿童能理解的话解释

**产品原则**
这款 App 的重点不是“给孩子一个强大的 AI 控制台”，而是“把强大的能力变成安全、清楚、可学习的创作按钮”。

因此界面上不要出现太多技术词，比如 API、Skill、Model、Workspace。可以替换成：

| 技术词 | 儿童界面文案 |
|---|---|
| Skill | 能力 |
| Prompt | 告诉 AI 你的想法 |
| Workspace | 我的作品文件夹 |
| Execute | 开始制作 |
| Diff | 看看改了什么 |
| Export | 保存作品 |
| Model | AI 大脑 |

**一句话版本**
这是一个儿童 AI 创作工作台：家长配置 DeepSeek API Key，孩子通过提示卡片调用聊天、PPT、网页和代码能力，在安全确认机制下完成自己的学习和创作作品。

---
name: swiftpm-macos
description: Build, run, and test pure SwiftPM-based macOS packages and executables. Use when the repo is package-first, when there is no Xcode project, or when Swift package workflows are the fastest path to diagnosis.
---

# SwiftPM for macOS

## Quick Start

Use this skill when `Package.swift` is the primary entrypoint or when SwiftPM is
the fastest path to a reproducible result.

## Workflow

1. Inspect the package.
   - Read `Package.swift`.
   - Identify executable, library, and test products.

2. Build with SwiftPM.
   - Use `swift build` by default.
   - Use release mode only when the user explicitly needs it.

3. Run the right product.
   - Use `swift run <product>` when an executable exists.
   - If multiple executables exist, explain the default choice.

4. Test narrowly.
   - Use `swift test`.
   - Apply filters when a specific test target or case is known.

5. Summarize failures.
   - Module/import resolution
   - Package graph or dependency issue
   - Linker failure
   - Runtime failure
   - Test regression

## Guardrails

- Prefer SwiftPM over Xcode when both exist and the package path is clearly simpler.
- Do not assume an app bundle exists in a pure package workflow.
- Explain when the package is library-only and therefore not directly runnable.

## Output Expectations

Provide:
- the package products you found
- the command you ran
- whether build, run, or test succeeded
- the top blocker if not

name: build-run-debug
description: Build, run, and debug local macOS apps and desktop executables using shell-first Xcode and Swift workflows. Use when asked to build a Mac app, launch it, diagnose compiler or linker failures, inspect startup problems, or debug desktop-only runtime issues.
---

# Build / Run / Debug

## Quick Start

Use this skill to set up one project-local `script/build_and_run.sh` entrypoint,
wire `.codex/environments/environment.toml` so the Codex app shows a Run button,
then use that script as the default build/run path.

Prefer shell-first workflows:

- `./script/build_and_run.sh` as the single kill + build + run entrypoint once it exists
- `xcodebuild` for Xcode workspaces or projects
- `swift build` plus raw executable launch inside that script for true SwiftPM command-line tools
- `swift build` plus project-local `.app` bundle staging and `/usr/bin/open -n` launch for SwiftPM AppKit/SwiftUI GUI apps
- optional script flags for `lldb`, `log stream`, telemetry verification, or post-launch process checks

Do not assume simulators, touch interaction, or mobile-specific tooling.

If an Xcode-aware MCP surface is already available and the user explicitly wants
it, use it only where it fits. Keep that usage narrow and honest: prefer it for
Xcode-oriented discovery, logging, or debugging support, and do not force
simulator-specific workflows onto pure macOS tasks.

## Workflow

1. Discover the project shape.
   - Check whether the workspace is already inside a git repo with `git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree`.
   - If no git repo is present, run `git init` at the project/workspace root before building so Codex app git-backed features are available. Never run `git init` inside a nested subdirectory when the current workspace already belongs to a parent repo.
   - Look for `.xcworkspace`, `.xcodeproj`, and `Package.swift<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1036" />y available, prefer it over ad hoc setup.
   - Use the MCP for Xcode-aware discovery or debug/logging workflows when the available tool surface clearly matches the task.
   - Fall back to shell commands immediately when the MCP does not provide a clean macOS path.

## Preferred Commands

- Project discovery:
  - `find . -name '*.xcworkspace' -o -name '*.xcodeproj' -o -name 'Package.swift'`
- Scheme discovery:
  - `xcodebuild -list -workspace <workspace>`
  - `xcodebuild -list -project <project>`
- Build/run:
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --debug`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --logs`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --telemetry`
  - `./script/build_and_run.sh --verify`

## References

- `references/run-button-bootstrap.md`: canonical `build_and_run.sh` and `.codex/environments/environment.toml` contract.

## Guardrails

- Prefer the narrowest command that proves or disproves the current theory.
- Do not leave the user with a one-off manual command chain once a stable `build_and_run.sh` script can own the workflow.
- Do not write `.codex/environments/environment.toml` before the run script exists, and do not point the Run action at a stale script path.
- Do not launch a SwiftUI/AppKit SwiftPM GUI app as a raw executable unless the user explicitly wants to diagnose that failure mode: it can produce no Dock icon, no foreground activation, and missing bundle identifier warnings. Keep raw executable launch only for true command-line tools.
- Do not claim UI state you cannot inspect directly.
- Do not describe mobile or simulator workflows as if they apply to macOS.
- If build output is huge, summarize the first real blocker and point to follow-up commands.

## Output Expectations

Provide:
- the detected project type
- the script path and Codex environment action you configured, if applicable
- the command you ran
- whether build and launch succeeded
- the top blocker if they failed
- the smallest sensible next action

name: Presentations
description: Build premium editorial analytics PPTX decks with artifact-tool presentation JSX, using ruthless narrative editing, chart-first storytelling, rendered critique, and iteration until the output beats the reference deck.
---

# Presentations

`Presentations` is for serious, high-polish presentation work where "clean" is
not enough. The target is an editable PowerPoint deck that feels like a strong
editor, a strong analyst, and a strong designer built it together.

Use this skill for analytics narratives, investor/operating reviews, strategy
stories, product/business performance decks, and any PPTX task where the user
asks to beat a reference deck.

## Operating Contract

Use artifact-tool presentation JSX only. The bundled Codex runtime provides
`@oai/artifact-tool` version 2.7.3 or newer and exposes
`@oai/artifact-tool/presentation-jsx`.

Do not require or import a separate presentation runtime package. PPTX
export/render behavior must be accessed through artifact-tool.

For generated raster images or reference comps, use the Codex imagegen tool.
Bundled scripts may write imagegen prompt files, but must not call external
image APIs or read API keys.

Thread-scoped paths:

- `SKILL_DIR=<absolute path to the installed Presentations skill>`
- `THREAD_ID=${CODEX_THREAD_ID:-manual-<timestamp-or-short-random-suffix>}`
- `WORKSPACE=$PWD/outputs/$THREAD_ID/presentations/<task-slug>`
- `SLIDES_DIR=$WORKSPACE/slides`
- `PREVIEW_DIR=$WORKSPACE/preview`
- `LAYOUT_DIR=$WORKSPACE/layout`
- `ASSET_DIR=$WORKSPACE/assets`
- `QA_DIR=$WORKSPACE/qa`
- `OUTPUT_DIR=<user-provided output dir if any, otherwise $WORKSPACE/output>`
- `FINAL_PPTX=$OUTPUT_DIR/<relevant-deck-title-slug>.pptx`

Use absolute paths in commands and handoffs. Keep all generated planning text
notes, preview PNGs, contact sheets, layout JSON, imagegen prompt f<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="421" />rt the final PPTX only after the rendered deck beats the target or the
    remaining gap is explicitly documented.
11. Clean up generated planning text notes, QA scratch files, contact sheets,
    preview images, generated slide modules/scripts, layout JSON, manifests,
    prompt text files, and other temporary images before the final response.

### Task Modes

- `reference-beating`: user supplied or named a deck to beat. This is the
  default when a reference PPTX is present.
- `template-following`: user supplied a source/template deck whose slide
  skeletons and visual system must be inherited.
- `create`: no deck/template is supplied; build from prompt and sources.
- `targeted-edit`: small changes to an existing deck.

When both a source deck and a better reference deck exist, separate them:

- source deck: content, required sections, facts, existing material.
- reference deck: quality bar, rhythm, taste, and proof that a stronger output
  is possible.

Do not blindly clone the reference. Beat it by improving story precision,
composition variety, chart clarity, whitespace, and final render quality.

### Deck Profile Router

After task mode, choose exactly one primary `deck-profile`. This is a hard
routing step, not a labeling exercise. The profile determines which proof
objects, source rules, visual density, asset rules, and QA gates are blocking.

Profiles:

- `finance-ir`: earnings, investor relations, operating reviews, financial
  analysis. Requires exact reported figures, unit discipline, source footnotes,
  bridges, tables, and disclosure logic.
- `product-platform`: SaaS/platform/product narratives. Requires architecture
  maps, workflow diagrams, adoption proof, product-to-financial linkage, and
  no generic feature-card grids.
- `gtm-growth`: GTM, marketing, consumer growth, subscription ecosystems,
  mobility, customer engagement. Requires a visible growth loop, segment or
  cohort proof, monetization bridge, and brand-aware rhythm.

name: utility-slideshow-creator

<!-- PM-Skills | https://github.com/product-on-purpose/pm-skills | Apache 2.0 -->

# Slideshow Creator

Generate professional presentations (.pptx and .pdf) from a JSON deck specification. Zero design decisions at generation time . Claude selects slide types and fills content slots; all visual properties are pre-decided by the theme.

## When to Use

- Creating slide decks for stakeholder updates, product reviews, team presentations
- Generating professional .pptx files from content briefs
- Producing consistent presentations without manual formatting
- Exporting presentations to PDF for sharing or archiving

## When NOT to Use

- Creating complex data visualizations (use dedicated charting tools)
- Building interactive web presentations (this produces .pptx, not HTML)
- Editing existing PowerPoint files (this creates new decks from scratch)

## How It Works

**Two-phase architecture:**

1. **Spec phase (Claude)** . Read the content brief, select slide types using decision logic, write a JSON deck specification. Content slots have character limits calibrated to prevent overflow. This is the only phase that costs tokens.
2. **Generation phase (Local script)** . Run `node scripts/generate-deck.js deck-spec.json` to produce .pptx. Optionally run `node scripts/export-pdf.mjs deck-spec.json` for PDF. Deterministic rendering, zero token cost.

Both <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="336" />ng between topics | `section` |
| Paragraph explanation | `content` |
| List of 3-6 points | `bullets` |
| Side-by-side comparison | `two_col` |
| Single key metric | `stat` |
| Two metrics compared | `dual_stat` |
| Testimonial or pull quote | `quote` |
| Three parallel concepts | `three_card` |
| Four concepts in a grid | `four_grid` |
| Dates or milestones | `timeline` |
| Sequential workflow | `process_flow` |
| Meeting agenda | `agenda` |
| Key finding or summary | `highlight` |
| Tabular data | `table` |
| Feature list with markers | `icon_rows` |
| Ending the deck | `closing` |

Full decision logic with variant strategy: `references/decision-logic.md`

## JSON Deck Spec Format

```json
{
  "title": "Q3 Product Update",
  "author": "Product Team",
  "footerText": "Internal . Q3 Review",
  "slides": [
    { "type": "title_dark", "title": "Q3 Product Update", "subtitle": "October 2026" },
    { "type": "stat", "stat": "94%", "label": "Customer satisfaction score", "accentColor": "secondary" },
    { "type": "bullets", "title": "What Shipped", "bullets": ["Feature A", "Feature B", "Feature C"] },
    { "type": "closing" }
  ]
}
```

Colors accept theme token names (`"accent"`, `"secondary"`, `"tertiary"`, `"warm"`) or 6-character hex strings (`"2563EB"`).

Full schema and workflow: `references/TEMPLATE.md` and `references/platform-rules.md`

## Instructions

1. **Read the content brief** . Understand topic, audience, length, specific requirements
2. **Plan the deck** . Select slide types using the decision logic table. Assign dark/light variants for visual rhythm (alternate to avoid monotony).
3. **Write the JSON deck specification** . Fill content slots, respecting character limits from `references/slide-types.md`
4. **Run the generation script** . `node scripts/generate-deck.js deck-spec.json`
5. **Optionally export PDF** . `node scripts/export-pdf.mjs deck-spec.json output.pdf`
6. **Report the output** . Tell the user where the file(s) are

name: frontend-app-builder
description: Use for new frontend applications, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, and visually driven UI from scratch, or when the user explicitly asks for a redesign/restyle/modernization. Builds from clean, airy, high-taste, readable image-generated concept design with section-specific references, faithful implementation, and browser testing.
---

# Frontend App Builder

Use this skill to create polished frontend apps, dashboards, games, creative websites, hero sections, redesigns, and other visually driven UI. Act first as a senior front-end designer, then as an engineer implementing an approved design spec.

## Core Standard

The two priorities of this skill outrank everything else:

1. Create enough great-looking Image Gen design first: clean, airy, distinctive, complete, readable, section-specific when needed, and not repetitive by default.
2. Do not stop until the accepted design and browser implementation match 10/10. Keep fixing visual, interaction, responsive, asset, and typography mismatches until `view_image` comparison would pass agency sign-off.

## Hard Rules

1. Use Image Gen for the visual concept unless the user explicitly opts out or the task is a small UI fix inside an existing design system.
2. Design the complete requested surface before coding. For a full page, app, dashboard, game, or product interface, a header or hero concept is not enough. For multi-section websites and long landing pages, prefer coordinated section-by-section concepts, plus an optional overview for rhythm, over one tall image that loses detail. For apps, dashboards, games, or compact product surfaces, generate the full primary screen plus any needed state, responsive, or asset concepts first.
3. Inside Codex, default multi-section website concepting to one fresh, large, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2040" />eliberately. Do not rely on browser defaults or inherited `16px` sizing for buttons, tabs, inputs, toolbars, sidebars, inspector panels, layer rows, status bars, command palettes, or export/share controls.
- Preserve the container model. Do not add cards, bordered panels, floating containers, tiles, or card grids where the spec uses open whitespace, bands, rails, lists, tables, canvases, or full-bleed composition.
- Keep real interactive app UI text, navigation, buttons, forms, tables, controls, and labels code-native. This does not apply to text and branding that belong inside product images, posters, packaging, signs, background scenes, hero photos, or other raster assets. Do not ship a static screenshot as UI.
- Use Image Gen for central non-icon assets. Render product images and background assets completely with the needed text, logos, marks, labels, packaging, signage, and branding. When the asset must layer into the UI, request a transparent background or clean cutout. Quote exact asset text and require verbatim rendering when text matters.
- If the accepted design includes branded product imagery, use Image Gen editing to create standalone versions of the logo/product/packaging/signage assets from the concept or a matching asset pass. Include transparent-background variants when those assets need to layer into the UI. Do not rebuild branded raster assets from generic CSS, mismatched fonts, or approximate labels.
- For games, use Image Gen for visible production art: character/state sprites or sprite sheets, terrain/platform tiles, collectibles, hazards, goals/checkpoints, foreground props, and parallax/background layers. Do not fall back to canvas-drawn shapes because collision, scaling, or animation is simpler. Keep HUD text, score, controls, hit boxes, physics, and game state code-native, and tune collision geometry to the rendered assets. Any code-drawn or vector game art must be listed as an intentional deviation or concrete blocker.

*** Add File: KidCreatorMac/script/build_and_run.sh
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
+PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
+DIST="$ROOT/dist"
+BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
+EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"
+
+cd "$ROOT"
+
+pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
+
+swift build
+
+rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
+mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
+cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"
+
+cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
+<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
+<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
+<plist version="1.0">
+<dict>
+  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
+  <string>$APP_NAME</string>
+  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
+  <string>local.kidcreator.mac</string>
+  <key>CFBundleName</key>
+  <string>Kid Creator</string>
+  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
+  <string>APPL</string>
+  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
+  <string>0.1.0</string>
+  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
+  <string>1</string>
+  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
+  <string>13.0</string>
+  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
+  <string>NSApplication</string>
+</dict>
+</plist>
+PLIST
+
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
+  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
+  sleep 2
+  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
+  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
+else
+  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
+fi

+# Kid Creator Mac
+
+Kid Creator Mac is a macOS SwiftUI prototype for a child-friendly AI creation workspace.
+
+The app uses a parent/teacher-provided DeepSeek API key and exposes four simple creation modes:
+
+- Ask: child-friendly chat and learning explanations.
+- Make PPT: prompts a local presentation skill adapter for slide outlines, speaking notes, and visual suggestions.
+- Make Website: prompts a local frontend skill adapter for safe single-page website plans and code.
+- Learn Code: explains attached code files and proposes safe code changes.
+
+## Run
+
+```bash
+./script/build_and_run.sh
+```
+
+The script builds the SwiftPM executable, stages `dist/KidCreatorMac.app`, and launches it.
+
+## Safety Model
+
+- DeepSeek API keys are stored in macOS Keychain.
+- The child-facing UI avoids exposing raw model or skill internals.
+- The app reminds children not to enter real names, school, address, phone numbers, or passwords.
+- File reads are explicit through the macOS file picker.
+- The current prototype does not silently write files or execute shell commands.
+
+## Local Skill Adapters
+
+The first version packages skill access as capability cards and prompt adapters:
+
+- `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium/SKILL.md`
+- `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/frontend-app-builder-pro/SKILL.md`
+
+Future iterations can replace the prompt adapters with real local skill runners and explicit export flows.
*** End Patch

import Foundation

@MainActor
final class AppStore: ObservableObject {
    @Published var selectedMode: CreatorMode = .chat {
        didSet { messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode) }
    }
    @Published var messages: [ChatMessage] = []
    @Published var draft = ""
    @Published var apiKey = ""
    @Published var model = "deepseek-chat"
    @Published var statusText = "请输入 DeepSeek API Key"
    @Published var isSending = false
    @Published var attachedFile: AttachedFile?
    @Published var lastError: String?

    private let client = DeepSeekClient()
    private let keychain = KeychainStore()
    private let conversationStore = ConversationStore()

    var canSend: Bool {
        !apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !draft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !isSending
    }

    init() {
        apiKey = (try? keychain.load()) ?? ""
        statusText = apiKey.isEmpty ? "请输入 DeepSeek API Key" : "DeepSeek Key 已保存"
        messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode)
    }

    func saveAPIKey() {
        do {
            try keychain.save(apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines))
            statusText = "DeepSeek Key 已保存到 Keychain"
            lastError = nil
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    func validateAPIKey() async {
        saveAPIKey()
        guard !apiKey.isEmpty else { return }
        isSending = true
        defer { isSending = false }

        do {
            _ = try await client.send(
                apiKey: apiKey,
                model: model,
                messages: [
                    ChatMessage(role: .system, content: "Reply with a short Chinese confirmation."),
                    ChatMessage(role: .user, content: "请回复<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="259" /> userContent)
        messages.append(userMessage)
        draft = ""
        isSending = true
        lastError = nil
        conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)

        do {
            let response = try await client.send(
                apiKey: apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines),
                model: model,
                messages: buildModelMessages()
            )
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role: .assistant, content: response))
            conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role: .assistant, content: "我现在没有成功连接到 DeepSeek。请让家长或老师检查 API Key 和网络。\n\n错误：\(error.localizedDescription)"))
            conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)
        }

        isSending = false
    }

    private func buildModelMessages() -> [ChatMessage] {
        let recentMessages = messages.suffix(12)
        let safety = """
        Important safety rules: The user may be a child. Keep content age-appropriate, educational, and encouraging. Do not ask for private personal information. For local files, explain before changing. Do not claim actions were executed outside this chat.
        """
        return [
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: selectedMode.systemPrompt),
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: safety)
        ] + recentMessages
    }

    private func buildUserContent(prompt: String) -> String {
        guard let attachedFile else { return prompt }

        let truncation = attachedFile.wasTruncated ? "\n\n注意：文件内容太长，下面只包含前一部分。" : ""
        return """
        \(prompt)

        附件文件：\(attachedFile.name)
        路径：\(attachedFile.path)\(truncation)

        ```text
        \(attachedFile.content)
        ```
        """
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct CapabilityInspectorView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    private var activeCapability: SkillCapability? {
        SkillCapability.all.first { capability in
            switch (capability.id, store.selectedMode) {
            case (.chat, .chat), (.slides, .slides), (.website, .website), (.code, .code):
                return true
            default:
                return false
            }
        }
    }

    var body: some View {
        ScrollView {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 18) {
                APIKeyPanelView()

                if let activeCapability {
                    CapabilityCardView(capability: activeCapability)
                }

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                    Text("儿童安全提示")
                        .font(.headline)
                    SafetyRow(icon: "person.crop.circle.badge.questionmark", text: "不要输入真实姓名、学校、地址、电话或密码。")
                    SafetyRow(icon: "checkmark.shield", text: "生成文件、保存作品、执行任务前需要家长或老师确认。")
                    SafetyRow(icon: "folder", text: "作品优先保存在本地，默认不上传整个文件夹。")
                }
                .padding()
                .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
            }
            .padding(18)
        }
        .navigationTitle("能力")
    }
}

private struct APIKeyPanelView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            Text("DeepSeek")
                .font(.headline)

            SecureField("由家长或老师输入 API Key", text: $store.apiKey)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)

            TextField("模型", text: $store.model)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)

            HStack {
                Button("保存") {
                    store.saveAPIKey()
                }

                Button("测试连接") {
                    Task { await store.validateAPIKey() }
                }
                .disabled(store.apiKey.isEmpty || store.isSending)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
    }
}

private struct CapabilityCardView: View {
    let capability: SkillCapability

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Label(capability.childName, systemImage: "wand.and.stars")
                .font(.headline)
            Text(capability.summary)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            Text(capability.safetyNote)
                .font(.callout)
            if let path = capability.localSkillPath {
                Text(path)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
                    .textSelection(.enabled)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
    }
}

private struct SafetyRow: View {
    let icon: String
    let text: String

    var body: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: icon)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .frame(width: 18)
            Text(text)
                .font(.callout)
        }
    }
}

import SwiftUI
import AppKit

struct ChatWorkspaceView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore
    @State private var reader = WorkspaceFileReader()

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            HeaderView()

            Divider()

            ScrollViewReader { proxy in
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
                        if store.messages.isEmpty {
                            EmptyModeView()
                        } else {
                            ForEach(store.messages) { message in
                                MessageBubbleView(message: message)
                                    .id(message.id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(24)
                }
                .onChange(of: store.messages.count) { _ in
                    if let last = store.messages.last {
                        withAnimation { proxy.scrollTo(last.id, anchor: .bottom) }
                    }
                }
            }

            Divider()
            ComposerView(onAttachFile: attachFile)
        }
        .navigationTitle(store.selectedMode.title)
    }

    private func attachFile() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        panel.canChooseFiles = true
        panel.allowedContentTypes = [.plainText, .sourceCode, .json, .html, .xml]

        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            do {
                store.attachFile(try reader.readTextFile(url: url))
            } catch {
                store.lastError = error.localizedDescription
            }
        }
    }
}

private struct HeaderView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    var body:<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="687" />                      Text("已截取前 20,000 字")

import Foundation

enum CreatorMode: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Codable {
    case chat
    case slides
    case website
    case code

    var id: String { rawValue }

    var title: String {
        switch self {
        case .chat: "问一问"
        case .slides: "做 PPT"
        case .website: "做网页"
        case .code: "学代码"
        }
    }

    var subtitle: String {
        switch self {
        case .chat: "把难问题讲简单"
        case .slides: "把主题变成课堂演示"
        case .website: "把想法变成网页作品"
        case .code: "解释代码和生成小程序"
        }
    }

    var systemImage: String {
        switch self {
        case .chat: "bubble.left.and.bubble.right"
        case .slides: "rectangle.on.rectangle.angled"
        case .website: "safari"
        case .code: "chevron.left.forwardslash.chevron.right"
        }
    }

    var actionTitle: String {
        switch self {
        case .chat: "发送问题"
        case .slides: "制作 PPT 方案"
        case .website: "制作网页方案"
        case .code: "分析代码"
        }
    }

    var systemPrompt: String {
        switch self {
        case .chat:
            return """
            You are a child-friendly learning assistant inside a macOS app. Explain ideas in simple Chinese, use short paragraphs, and ask one helpful follow-up question when useful. Do not ask the child for private information such as full name, school, address, phone number, or passwords.
            """
        case .slides:
            return """
            You are using the local PPT skill adapter. Create a child-friendly presentation plan in Chinese. Return: title, audience, slide-by-slide outline, simple speaking notes, and image suggestions. Keep language suitable for children. Do not claim that a PPTX file was exported unless t<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="262" />个关于海洋动物的小作品，请帮我想 5 个有趣主题。"),
                PromptCard(title: "出题考我", prompt: "请用简单问题考我关于恐龙的知识，每次只问一个问题。")
            ]
        case .slides:
            return [
                PromptCard(title: "恐龙课堂 PPT", prompt: "帮我做一个 5 页 PPT，主题是恐龙，给小学同学看，语言要简单有趣。"),
                PromptCard(title: "英语演讲", prompt: "帮我做一个 4 页英文演讲 PPT，主题是 My Favorite Animal，每页有短句和演讲稿。"),
                PromptCard(title: "科学展示", prompt: "帮我做一个关于太阳系的 6 页 PPT，要有每页标题、3 个要点和配图建议。")
            ]
        case .website:
            return [
                PromptCard(title: "个人作品页", prompt: "帮我做一个儿童个人作品网页，不要填写真实姓名，用昵称，展示绘画、故事和小目标。"),
                PromptCard(title: "动物百科", prompt: "帮我设计一个海洋动物百科网页，有 3 个动物卡片、颜色活泼、适合小朋友阅读。"),
                PromptCard(title: "数学小游戏", prompt: "帮我做一个简单数学练习网页，可以出 10 以内加法题，并告诉我答对了几题。")
            ]
        case .code:
            return [
                PromptCard(title: "解释代码", prompt: "请解释我附上的代码，先告诉我它是做什么的，再用简单例子说明。"),
                PromptCard(title: "做小游戏", prompt: "请帮我写一个简单 HTML 小游戏：点击星星得分，30 秒结束。"),
                PromptCard(title: "一步步教我", prompt: "请一步一步教我写一个 JavaScript 计数器，每一步只讲一个概念。")
            ]
        }
    }
}

struct PromptCard: Identifiable, Codable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var title: String
    var prompt: String
}

+# Kid Creator Mac
+
+Kid Creator Mac is a macOS SwiftUI prototype for a child-friendly AI creation workspace.
+
+The app uses a parent/teacher-provided DeepSeek API key and exposes four simple creation modes:
+
+- Ask: child-friendly chat and learning explanations.
+- Make PPT: prompts a local presentation skill adapter for slide outlines, speaking notes, and visual suggestions.
+- Make Website: prompts a local frontend skill adapter for safe single-page website plans and code.
+- Learn Code: explains attached code files and proposes safe code changes.
+
+## Run
+
+```bash
+./script/build_and_run.sh
+```
+
+The script builds the SwiftPM executable, stages `dist/KidCreatorMac.app`, and launches it.
+
+## Safety Model
+
+- DeepSeek API keys are stored in macOS Keychain.
+- The child-facing UI avoids exposing raw model or skill internals.
+- The app reminds children not to enter real names, school, address, phone numbers, or passwords.
+- File reads are explicit through the macOS file picker.
+- The current prototype does not silently write files or execute shell commands.
+
+## Local Skill Adapters
+
+The first version packages skill access as capability cards and prompt adapters:
+
+- `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium/SKILL.md`
+- `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/frontend-app-builder-pro/SKILL.md`
+
+Future iterations can replace the prompt adapters with real local skill runners and explicit export flows.
*** End Patch

import Foundation

@MainActor
final class AppStore: ObservableObject {
    @Published var selectedMode: CreatorMode = .chat {
        didSet { messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode) }
    }
    @Published var messages: [ChatMessage] = []
    @Published var draft = ""
    @Published var apiKey = ""
    @Published var model = "deepseek-chat"
    @Published var statusText = "请输入 DeepSeek API Key"
    @Published var isSending = false
    @Published var attachedFile: AttachedFile?
    @Published var lastError: String?

    private let client = DeepSeekClient()
    private let keychain = KeychainStore()
    private let conversationStore = ConversationStore()

    var canSend: Bool {
        !apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !draft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !isSending
    }

    init() {
        apiKey = (try? keychain.load()) ?? ""
        statusText = apiKey.isEmpty ? "请输入 DeepSeek API Key" : "DeepSeek Key 已保存"
        messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode)
    }

    func saveAPIKey() {
        do {
            try keychain.save(apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines))
            statusText = "DeepSeek Key 已保存到 Keychain"
            lastError = nil
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }

    func validateAPIKey() async {
        saveAPIKey()
        guard !apiKey.isEmpty else { return }
        isSending = true
        defer { isSending = false }

        do {
            _ = try await client.send(
                apiKey: apiKey,
                model: model,
                messages: [
                    ChatMessage(role: .system, content: "Reply with a short Chinese confirmation."),
                    ChatMessage(role: .user, content: "请回复<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="259" /> userContent)
        messages.append(userMessage)
        draft = ""
        isSending = true
        lastError = nil
        conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)

        do {
            let response = try await client.send(
                apiKey: apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines),
                model: model,
                messages: buildModelMessages()
            )
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role: .assistant, content: response))
            conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)
        } catch {
            lastError = error.localizedDescription
            messages.append(ChatMessage(role: .assistant, content: "我现在没有成功连接到 DeepSeek。请让家长或老师检查 API Key 和网络。\n\n错误：\(error.localizedDescription)"))
            conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)
        }

        isSending = false
    }

    private func buildModelMessages() -> [ChatMessage] {
        let recentMessages = messages.suffix(12)
        let safety = """
        Important safety rules: The user may be a child. Keep content age-appropriate, educational, and encouraging. Do not ask for private personal information. For local files, explain before changing. Do not claim actions were executed outside this chat.
        """
        return [
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: selectedMode.systemPrompt),
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: safety)
        ] + recentMessages
    }

    private func buildUserContent(prompt: String) -> String {
        guard let attachedFile else { return prompt }

        let truncation = attachedFile.wasTruncated ? "\n\n注意：文件内容太长，下面只包含前一部分。" : ""
        return """
        \(prompt)

        附件文件：\(attachedFile.name)
        路径：\(attachedFile.path)\(truncation)

        ```text
        \(attachedFile.content)
        ```
        """
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct CapabilityInspectorView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    private var activeCapability: SkillCapability? {
        SkillCapability.all.first { capability in
            switch (capability.id, store.selectedMode) {
            case (.chat, .chat), (.slides, .slides), (.website, .website), (.code, .code):
                return true
            default:
                return false
            }
        }
    }

    var body: some View {
        ScrollView {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 18) {
                APIKeyPanelView()

                if let activeCapability {
                    CapabilityCardView(capability: activeCapability)
                }

                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                    Text("儿童安全提示")
                        .font(.headline)
                    SafetyRow(icon: "person.crop.circle.badge.questionmark", text: "不要输入真实姓名、学校、地址、电话或密码。")
                    SafetyRow(icon: "checkmark.shield", text: "生成文件、保存作品、执行任务前需要家长或老师确认。")
                    SafetyRow(icon: "folder", text: "作品优先保存在本地，默认不上传整个文件夹。")
                }
                .padding()
                .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
            }
            .padding(18)
        }
        .navigationTitle("能力")
    }
}

private struct APIKeyPanelView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
            Text("DeepSeek")
                .font(.headline)

            SecureField("由家长或老师输入 API Key", text: $store.apiKey)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)

            TextField("模型", text: $store.model)
                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)

            HStack {
                Button("保存") {
                    store.saveAPIKey()
                }

                Button("测试连接") {
                    Task { await store.validateAPIKey() }
                }
                .disabled(store.apiKey.isEmpty || store.isSending)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
    }
}

private struct CapabilityCardView: View {
    let capability: SkillCapability

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Label(capability.childName, systemImage: "wand.and.stars")
                .font(.headline)
            Text(capability.summary)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            Text(capability.safetyNote)
                .font(.callout)
            if let path = capability.localSkillPath {
                Text(path)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
                    .textSelection(.enabled)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
    }
}

private struct SafetyRow: View {
    let icon: String
    let text: String

    var body: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: icon)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .frame(width: 18)
            Text(text)
                .font(.callout)
        }
    }
}

import SwiftUI
import AppKit

struct ChatWorkspaceView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore
    @State private var reader = WorkspaceFileReader()

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            HeaderView()

            Divider()

            ScrollViewReader { proxy in
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
                        if store.messages.isEmpty {
                            EmptyModeView()
                        } else {
                            ForEach(store.messages) { message in
                                MessageBubbleView(message: message)
                                    .id(message.id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(24)
                }
                .onChange(of: store.messages.count) { _ in
                    if let last = store.messages.last {
                        withAnimation { proxy.scrollTo(last.id, anchor: .bottom) }
                    }
                }
            }

            Divider()
            ComposerView(onAttachFile: attachFile)
        }
        .navigationTitle(store.selectedMode.title)
    }

    private func attachFile() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        panel.canChooseFiles = true
        panel.allowedContentTypes = [.plainText, .sourceCode, .json, .html, .xml]

        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            do {
                store.attachFile(try reader.readTextFile(url: url))
            } catch {
                store.lastError = error.localizedDescription
            }
        }
    }
}

private struct HeaderView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    var body:<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="687" />                      Text("已截取前 20,000 字")
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button {
                        store.clearAttachment()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.borderless)
                }
                .font(.caption)
                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
            }

            HStack(alignment: .bottom, spacing: 10) {
                TextEditor(text: $store.draft)
                    .font(.body)
                    .scrollContentBackground(.hidden)
                    .frame(minHeight: 70, maxHeight: 130)
                    .padding(8)
                    .background(.background, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
                    .overlay(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8).stroke(.quaternary))

                VStack(spacing: 8) {
                    if store.selectedMode == .code {
                        Button {
                            onAttachFile()
                        } label: {
                            Image(systemName: "paperclip")
                        }
                        .help("附加一个代码或文本文件")
                    }

                    Button {
                        Task { await store.sendDraft() }
                    } label: {
                        if store.isSending {
                            ProgressView()
                                .controlSize(.small)
                        } else {
                            Image(systemName: "paperplane.fill")
                        }
                    }
                    .keyboardShortcut(.return, modifiers: [.command])
                    .disabled(!store.canSend)
                    .help(store.selectedMode.actionTitle)
                }
            }

    }
}

# JSON Deck Specification Template

<!-- Use this template as a starting point for your deck specification. Fill in the content fields, respecting the character limits documented in slide-types.md. -->

## Deck Metadata

```json
{
  "title": "",
  "author": "",
  "footerText": "",
  "outputFileName": "",
  "slides": []
}
```

### Field Reference

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| title | string | yes | Presentation title, used as .pptx metadata |
| author | string | no | Author name for .pptx metadata |
| footerText | string | no | Default footer text for all slides |
| outputFileName | string | no | Output filename. Default: title slugified + .pptx |
| slides | array | yes | Array of slide objects |

## Slide Object Structure

Every slide object has `type` (required) plus content slots defined in `slide-types.md`.

### Common Optional Fields

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| variant | string | "dark" or "light" -- see defaults per type in slide-types.md |
| notes | string | Speaker notes for presenter view (no character limit) |
| footer | string | Override deck-level footerText for this slide |
| slideNum | string | Override auto-incremented slide number |

### Color Values

Color fields (`accentColor`, `leftColor`, `rightColor`, card/item `color`) accept:
- **Theme token names:** `"accent"`, `"secondary"`, `"tertiary"`, `"warm"`, `"primary"`, `"primaryDark"`, `"muted"`
- **6-character hex strings:** `"2563EB"`, `"0891B2"` (no `#` prefix)

## Slide Type Quick Reference

| Type Key | Required Slots | Optional Slots |
|---|---|---|
| title_dark | title | subtitle |
| title_light | title | subtitle |
| section | title | variant |
| content | title, body | variant |
| bullets | title, bullets[] | variant |
| two_col | title, leftTitle, leftItems[], rightTitle, rightItems[] | variant |
| stat | stat, label | accentColor, variant |
| dual_stat | leftStat, leftLabel, rightStat, rightLabel | title, leftColor, rightColor, variant |
| quote | quote | attribution, variant |
| three_card | cards[3] | title, variant |
| four_grid | cards[4] | title, variant |
| timeline | title, milestones[] | variant |
| process_flow | title, steps[] | variant |
| agenda | items[] | title, intro, variant |
| highlight | title, highlight | context, accentColor, variant |
| table | title, headers[], rows[][] | variant |
| icon_rows | title, items[] | variant |
| closing | (none) | tagline, url |

See `slide-types.md` for character limits and detailed slot definitions.

# JSON Deck Specification Template

<!-- Use this template as a starting point for your deck specification. Fill in the content fields, respecting the character limits documented in slide-types.md. -->

## Deck Metadata

```json
{
  "title": "",
  "author": "",
  "footerText": "",
  "outputFileName": "",
  "slides": []
}
```

### Field Reference

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| title | string | yes | Presentation title, used as .pptx metadata |
| author | string | no | Author name for .pptx metadata |
| footerText | string | no | Default footer text for all slides |
| outputFileName | string | no | Output filename. Default: title slugified + .pptx |
| slides | array | yes | Array of slide objects |

## Slide Object Structure

Every slide object has `type` (required) plus content slots defined in `slide-types.md`.

### Common Optional Fields

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| variant | string | "dark" or "light" -- see defaults per type in slide-types.md |
| notes | string | Speaker notes for presenter view (no character limit) |
| footer | string | Override deck-level footerText for this slide |
| slideNum | string | Override auto-incremented slide number |

### Color Values

Color fields (`accentColor`, `leftColor`, `rightColor`, card/item `color`) accept:
- **Theme token names:** `"accent"`, `"secondary"`, `"tertiary"`, `"warm"`, `"primary"`, `"primaryDark"`, `"muted"`
- **6-character hex strings:** `"2563EB"`, `"0891B2"` (no `#` prefix)

## Slide Type Quick Reference

| Type Key | Required Slots | Optional Slots |
|---|---|---|
| title_dark | title | subtitle |
| title_light | title | subtitle |
| section | title | variant |
| content | title, body | variant |
| bullets | title, bullets[] | variant |
| two_col | title, leftTitle, leftItems[], rightTitle, rightItems[] | variant |
| stat | stat, label | accentColor, variant |
| dual_stat | leftStat, leftLabel, rightStat, rightLabel | title, leftColor, rightColor, variant |
| quote | quote | attribution, variant |
| three_card | cards[3] | title, variant |
| four_grid | cards[4] | title, variant |
| timeline | title, milestones[] | variant |
| process_flow | title, steps[] | variant |
| agenda | items[] | title, intro, variant |
| highlight | title, highlight | context, accentColor, variant |
| table | title, headers[], rows[][] | variant |
| icon_rows | title, items[] | variant |
| closing | (none) | tagline, url |

See `slide-types.md` for character limits and detailed slot definitions.

 Future iterations can replace the prompt adapters with real local skill runners and explicit export flows.
+
+## Artifact Export
+
+Saved works are written to `~/Documents/Kid Creator Works`.
+
+- Website mode saves `index.html` and opens it for local preview.
+- PPT mode saves `presentation.md`, `slides-preview.html`, and extracts `deck-spec.json` when the AI response includes a valid JSON deck spec.
+- Chat and Code modes save Markdown notes.
*** End Patch
