+private struct PromptCoachPanelView: View {
+    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore
+    @State private var topic = ""
+    @State private var audience = "小学同学"
+    @State private var style = "有趣"
+    @State private var detail = ""
+
+    var body: some View {
+        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
+            Text("提示助手")
+                .font(.headline)
+
+            Text("填几个简单选项，App 会帮你组成完整请求。")
+                .font(.callout)
+                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
+
+            TextField(topicPlaceholder, text: $topic)
+                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
+
+            Picker("给谁看", selection: $audience) {
+                ForEach(audienceOptions, id: \.self) { option in
+                    Text(option).tag(option)
+                }
+            }
+            .pickerStyle(.menu)
+
+            Picker("风格", selection: $style) {
+                ForEach(styleOptions, id: \.self) { option in
+                    Text(option).tag(option)
+                }
+            }
+            .pickerStyle(.segmented)
+
+            TextField("还想补充什么？", text: $detail, axis: .vertical)
+                .textFieldStyle(.roundedBorder)
+                .lineLimit(2...4)
+
+            Button("生成提示") {
+                store.draft = makePrompt()
+            }
+            .disabled(topic.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
+        }
+        .padding()
+        .background(.thinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
+    }
+
+    private var topicPlaceholder: Stri<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="205" /> {
+        case .chat: return ["简单", "有趣", "故事"]
+        case .slides: return ["清楚", "有趣", "彩色"]
+        case .website: return ["可爱", "清爽", "活泼"]
+        case .code: return ["一步步", "简单", "挑战"]
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func makePrompt() -> String {
+        let cleanTopic = topic.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+        let extra = detail.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+        let suffix = extra.isEmpty ? "" : "\n补充要求：\(extra)"
+
+        switch store.selectedMode {
+        case .chat:
+            return "请用\(style)的方式，讲给\(audience)听：\(cleanTopic)。每段短一点，可以举例子。\(suffix)"
+        case .slides:
+            return """
+            请制作一个适合\(audience)的 5 页 PPT 方案，主题是：\(cleanTopic)。
+            风格：\(style)。
+            请包含：标题、每页标题、每页 3 个要点、每页演讲稿、配图建议。
+            最后请输出一个 ```json 代码块，格式包含 title、author、footerText、slides 数组，方便保存为 deck-spec.json。
+            \(suffix)
+            """
+        case .website:
+            return """
+            请制作一个适合\(audience)的单页网页，主题是：\(cleanTopic)。
+            风格：\(style)。
+            要求：安全、适合儿童、不要收集真实姓名/学校/地址/电话。
+            请输出一个完整的 ```html 代码块，CSS 和 JavaScript 都写在同一个 HTML 文件里。
+            \(suffix)
+            """
+        case .code:
+            return """
+            请用\(style)的方式教\(audience)：\(cleanTopic)。
+            先解释核心概念，再给一个可以运行的小例子，最后列出 3 个可以自己改的小挑战。
+            \(suffix)
+            """
+        }
+    }
+}
 
 private struct ExportPanelView: View {
*** End Patch

import SwiftUI
import AppKit

struct ChatWorkspaceView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore
    @State private var reader = WorkspaceFileReader()

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            HeaderView()

            Divider()

            ScrollViewReader { proxy in
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
                        if store.messages.isEmpty {
                            EmptyConversationView()
                        } else {
                            ForEach(store.messages) { message in
                                MessageBubbleView(message: message)
                                    .id(message.id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(24)
                }
                .onChange(of: store.messages.count) { _ in
                    if let last = store.messages.last {
                        withAnimation { proxy.scrollTo(last.id, anchor: .bottom) }
                    }
                }
            }

            Divider()
            ComposerView(onAttachFile: attachFile)
        }
        .navigationTitle(store.selectedMode.title)
    }

    private func attachFile() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        panel.canChooseFiles = true
        panel.allowedContentTypes = [.plainText, .sourceCode, .json, .html, .xml]

        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            do {
                store.attachFile(try reader.readTextFile(url: url))
            } catch {
                store.lastError = error.localizedDescription
            }
        }
    }
}

private struct HeaderView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

    v<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="437" />                      Text("已截取前 20,000 字")
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                    Spacer()
                    Button {
                        store.clearAttachment()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.borderless)
                }
                .font(.caption)
                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
            }

            HStack(alignment: .bottom, spacing: 10) {
                TextEditor(text: $store.draft)
                    .font(.body)
                    .scrollContentBackground(.hidden)
                    .frame(minHeight: 70, maxHeight: 130)
                    .padding(8)
                    .background(.background, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))

                            Image(systemName: "paperclip")

                        .help("附加一个代码或文本文件")
                    }

                    Button {
                        Task { await store.sendDraft() }
                    } label: {
                        if store.isSending {
                            ProgressView()
                                .controlSize(.small)
                        } else {
                            Image(systemName: "paperplane.fill")
                        }
                    }
                    .keyboardShortcut(.return, modifiers: [.command])
                    .disabled(!store.canSend)
                    .help(store.selectedMode.actionTitle)
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(16)
    }
}

import SwiftUI
import AppKit

struct ChatWorkspaceView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore
    @State private var reader = WorkspaceFileReader()

    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 0) {
            HeaderView()

            Divider()

            ScrollViewReader { proxy in
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 16) {
                        if store.messages.isEmpty {
                            EmptyConversationView()
                        } else {
                            ForEach(store.messages) { message in
                                MessageBubbleView(message: message)
                                    .id(message.id)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(24)
                }
                .onChange(of: store.messages.count) { _ in
                    if let last = store.messages.last {
                        withAnimation { proxy.scrollTo(last.id, anchor: .bottom) }
                    }
                }
            }

            Divider()
            ComposerView(onAttachFile: attachFile)
        }
        .navigationTitle(store.selectedMode.title)
    }

    private func attachFile() {
        let panel = NSOpenPanel()
        panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
        panel.canChooseDirectories = false
        panel.canChooseFiles = true
        panel.allowedContentTypes = [.plainText, .sourceCode, .json, .html, .xml]

        if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
            do {
                store.attachFile(try reader.readTextFile(url: url))
            } catch {
                store.lastError = error.localizedDescription
            }
        }
    }
}

    @EnvironmentObject private var store: AppStore

+import Foundation

+enum ModelProvider: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable, Codable {

+

---
name: packaging-notarization
description: Prepare and troubleshoot packaging, signing, and notarization workflows for macOS distribution. Use when asked to archive a Mac app, validate bundle structure, reason about notarization readiness, or explain distribution-only failures.
---

# Packaging & Notarization

## Quick Start

Use this skill when the work is about shipping the app rather than merely
running it locally: archives, exported app bundles, notarization readiness,
hardened runtime, or distribution validation.

## Workflow

1. Confirm the distribution goal.
   - Local archive validation
   - Signed distributable app
   - Notarization troubleshooting

2. Inspect the artifact.
   - Validate app bundle structure.
   - Check nested frameworks, helper tools, and entitlements.

3. Inspect signing and runtime prerequisites.
   - Hardened runtime
   - Signing identity
   - Nested code signatures
   - Required entitlements

4. Explain notarization readiness or failure.
   - Separate packaging issues from trust-policy symptoms.
   - Point to the minimum follow-up validation commands.

## Guardrails

- Do not present notarization as required for ordinary local debug runs.
- Call out when you lack the actual exported artifact and are inferring from project settings.
- Keep advice concrete and verifiable.

## Output Expectations

Provide:
- what artifact or settings were inspected
- whether the app looks distribution-ready
- the top missing prerequisite or failure mode
- the next validation or repair step

[325] assistant: 我会补一个 `package_dmg.sh` 脚本，先复用现有构建脚本生成 `.app`，再创建 DMG staging 目录和 Applications 链接，最后用 `hdiutil` 生成安装包。

[326] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: KidCreatorMac/script/package_dmg.sh
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+APP_NAME="Kid Creator"
+PROCESS_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
+VERSION="0.1.0"
+DIST="$ROOT/dist"
+APP_BUNDLE="$DIST/$PROCESS_NAME.app"
+STAGING="$DIST/dmg-staging"
+DMG_PATH="$DIST/Kid-Creator-$VERSION.dmg"
+
+cd "$ROOT"
+
+"$ROOT/script/build_and_run.sh" --no-open
+
+rm -rf "$STAGING" "$DMG_PATH"
+mkdir -p "$STAGING"
+
+cp -R "$APP_BUNDLE" "$STAGING/$APP_NAME.app"
+ln -s /Applications "$STAGING/Applications"
+
+hdiutil create \
+  -volname "$APP_NAME" \
+  -srcfolder "$STAGING" \
+  -ov \
+  -format UDZO \
+  "$DMG_PATH"
+
+echo "$DMG_PATH"
*** Update File: KidCreatorMac/script/build_and_run.sh
@@
-if [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
+  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
+elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
   /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
   sleep 2
   pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
*** Update File: KidCreatorMac/.gitignore
@@
 .build/
 dist/
 .swiftpm/
*** End Patch

[325] assistant: 我会补一个 `package_dmg.sh` 脚本，先复用现有构建脚本生成 `.app`，再创建 DMG staging 目录和 Applications 链接，最后用 `hdiutil` 生成安装包。

[326] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: KidCreatorMac/script/package_dmg.sh
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+set -euo pipefail
+
+ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
+APP_NAME="Kid Creator"
+PROCESS_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
+VERSION="0.1.0"
+DIST="$ROOT/dist"
+APP_BUNDLE="$DIST/$PROCESS_NAME.app"
+STAGING="$DIST/dmg-staging"
+DMG_PATH="$DIST/Kid-Creator-$VERSION.dmg"
+
+cd "$ROOT"
+
+"$ROOT/script/build_and_run.sh" --no-open
+
+rm -rf "$STAGING" "$DMG_PATH"
+mkdir -p "$STAGING"
+
+cp -R "$APP_BUNDLE" "$STAGING/$APP_NAME.app"
+ln -s /Applications "$STAGING/Applications"
+
+hdiutil create \
+  -volname "$APP_NAME" \
+  -srcfolder "$STAGING" \
+  -ov \
+  -format UDZO \
+  "$DMG_PATH"
+
+echo "$DMG_PATH"
*** Update File: KidCreatorMac/script/build_and_run.sh
@@
-if [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
+if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
+  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
+elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
   /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
   sleep 2
   pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
*** Update File: KidCreatorMac/.gitignore
@@
 .build/
 dist/
 .swiftpm/
*** End Patch

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
DIST="$ROOT/dist"
BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"

cd "$ROOT"

pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

swift build

rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"

cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>$APP_NAME</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>local.kidcreator.mac</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>Kid Creator</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>0.1.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>1</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>13.0</string>
  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
  <string>NSApplication</string>
</dict>
</plist>
PLIST

codesign --force --deep --sign - "$BUNDLE"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
  sleep 2
  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
else
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
DIST="$ROOT/dist"
BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"

cd "$ROOT"

pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

swift build

rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"

cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>$APP_NAME</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>local.kidcreator.mac</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>Kid Creator</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>0.1.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>1</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>13.0</string>
  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
  <string>NSApplication</string>
</dict>
</plist>
PLIST

codesign --force --deep --sign - "$BUNDLE"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
  sleep 2
  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
else
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
fi

[451] user: 只需要发送dmg到其他mac 笔记本就可以安装了吗


[452] assistant: 基本可以：把 `Kid-Creator-0.1.0.dmg` 发到其他 Mac，双击打开，然后把 `Kid Creator.app` 拖到 `Applications` 就能安装。

但注意一点：现在这个包是 **ad-hoc 签名，没有 Apple Developer ID 公证**。所以其他 Mac 第一次打开时可能会被 Gatekeeper 拦截，提示“无法验证开发者”。

解决方式：

1. 右键 `Kid Creator.app`
2. 选择 `打开`
3. 再点一次 `打开`

这样通常可以运行。

如果你要正式分发给很多用户，就需要：
- Apple Developer ID 签名
- Hardened Runtime
- Notarization 公证
- stapler 票据附加

自己测试或发给熟悉的人，直接发 DMG 就可以。

      };

import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import fsSync from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

export const DEFAULT_SLIDE_SIZE = { width: 1280, height: 720 };
const MIN_ARTIFACT_TOOL_VERSION = "2.7.3";

export function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = {};
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
    const key = argv[index];
    if (!key.startsWith("--")) {
      throw new Error(`Unexpected positional argument: ${key}`);
    }
    const value = argv[index + 1];
    if (!value || value.startsWith("--")) {
      args[key.slice(2)] = true;
      continue;
    }
    args[key.slice(2)] = value;
    index += 1;
  }
  return args;
}

export function requireArg(args, key) {
  const value = args[key];
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(`Missing required --${key}`);
  }
  return value;
}

export function parseSlideSize(value) {
  if (!value) return DEFAULT_SLIDE_SIZE;
  const match = String(value).match(/^(\d+)x(\d+)$/);
  if (!match) {
    throw new Error(`Expected slide size as WIDTHxHEIGHT, got: ${value}`);
  }
  return {
    width: Number.parseInt(match[1], 10),
    height: Number.parseInt(match[2], 10),
  };
}

export function padSlideNumber(value) {
  return String(value).padStart(2, "0");
}

export function slideNumberFromModuleName(filePath) {
  const base = path.basename(filePath);
  const match = base.match(/^slide[-_]?(\d+)\.mjs$/i);
  if (!match) return undefined;
  return Number.parseInt(match[1], 10);
}

function defaultRuntimeNodeModules() {
  return path.join(
    process.env.HOME || process.cwd(),
    ".cache",
    "codex-runtimes",
    ["codex", "primary", "runtime"].join("-"),
    "dependencies",
    "node",
    "node_modules",
  );
}

function packageJsonPath(packageDir) {
  return path.joi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="781" />T_TOOL_VERSION} or newer.`,
        `Checked ${packageDir}; found @oai/artifact-tool@${packageJson.version || "unknown"}.`,
      ].join("\n"),
    );
  }
  const entrypoint = artifactToolEntrypointPath(packageDir);
  if (!entrypoint) {
    throw new Error(
      [
        `Expected ${context} to include a built artifact-tool entrypoint.`,
        `Checked ${path.join(packageDir, "dist", "node", "artifact_tool.mjs")} and ${path.join(packageDir, "dist", "artifact_tool.mjs")}.`,
        "Build the local artifact-tool bundle before retrying.",
      ].join("\n"),
    );
  }
  validateArtifactToolPresentationJsx(packageDir, context);
  return { packageDir: path.resolve(packageDir), entrypoint: path.resolve(entrypoint) };
}

function findArtifactToolPackage() {
  return validateArtifactToolPackage(
    runtimePackagePath("@oai/artifact-tool"),
    "the bundled Codex runtime @oai/artifact-tool package",
  );
}

function runtimePackagePath(packageName) {
  return path.join(defaultRuntimeNodeModules(), ...packageName.split("/"));
}

function findOptionalRuntimePackage(packageName) {
  const runtimePackage = runtimePackagePath(packageName);
  if (isNamedPackage(runtimePackage, packageName)) {
    return runtimePackage;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName) {
  return path.join(workspaceDir, "node_modules", ...packageName.split("/"));
}

async function ensureWorkspacePackage(workspaceDir, packageName, sourcePackage) {
  const target = workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName);
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });

  const existing = await fs.lstat(target).catch(() => undefined);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      fsSync.existsSync(packageJsonPath(target)) &&
      isNamedPackage(target, packageName) &&
      isSamePackageLocation(target, sourcePackage)
    ) {
      return target;
    }
    if (existing.isSymbolicLink()) {
      await fs.rm(target, { recursive: true, force: true });

---
name: Presentations
description: Build premium editorial analytics PPTX decks with artifact-tool presentation JSX, using ruthless narrative editing, chart-first storytelling, rendered critique, and iteration until the output beats the reference deck.
---

# Presentations

`Presentations` is for serious, high-polish presentation work where "clean" is
not enough. The target is an editable PowerPoint deck that feels like a strong
editor, a strong analyst, and a strong designer built it together.

Use this skill for analytics narratives, investor/operating reviews, strategy
stories, product/business performance decks, and any PPTX task where the user
asks to beat a reference deck.

## Operating Contract

Use artifact-tool presentation JSX only. The bundled Codex runtime provides
`@oai/artifact-tool` version 2.7.3 or newer and exposes
`@oai/artifact-tool/presentation-jsx`.

Do not require or import a separate presentation runtime package. PPTX
export/render behavior must be accessed through artifact-tool.

For generated raster images or reference comps, use the Codex imagegen tool.
Bundled scripts may write imagegen prompt files, but must not call external
image APIs or read API keys.

Thread-scoped paths:

- `SKILL_DIR=<absolute path to the installed Presentations skill>`
- `THREAD_ID=${CODEX_THREAD_ID:-manual-<timestamp-or-short-random-suffix>}`
- `WORKSPACE=$PWD/outputs/$THREAD_ID/presentations/<task-slug>`
- `SLIDES_DIR=$WORKSPACE/slides`
- `PREVIEW_DIR=$WORKSPACE/preview`
- `LAYOUT_DIR=$WORKSPACE/layout`
- `ASSET_DIR=$WORKSPACE/assets`
- `QA_DIR=$WORKSPACE/qa`
- `OUTPUT_DIR=<user-provided output dir if any, otherwise $WORKSPACE/output>`
- `FINAL_PPTX=$OUTPUT_DIR/<relevant-deck-title-slug>.pptx`

Use absolute paths in commands and handoffs. Keep all generated planning text
notes, preview PNGs, contact sheets, layout JSON, imagegen prompt f<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1093" />ect. Requires real assets or explicit asset
  provenance, image quality, editorial hierarchy, and client-ready copy.
- `template-following`: user supplied a template/source deck whose slides must
  be treated as the editable starting point. Requires `template-audit.txt`,
  `template-frame-map.json`, and `deviation-log.txt`; follow
  `template-and-edit.md` for the exact duplicate-first starter method.
- `targeted-edit-data`: add or edit a data/comparison slide. Requires exact
  calculations before visual work and a native-looking insertion into the
  existing deck.
- `targeted-edit-media`: add headshots, logos, screenshots, or other media.
  Requires identity/source verification, consistent crops, and preservation of
  the source deck layout grammar.
- `appendix-heavy`: dense appendix, tables, disclosures, or source packs.
  Requires index/page markers, readable small-type thresholds, table grammar,
  and explicit source-density rules.

If more than one profile applies, pick the profile that creates the highest
delivery risk as primary, then list secondary gates in the claim spine. Example:
a finance investor deck with a supplied template is `finance-ir` primary and
`template-following` secondary.

When a profile is selected, read its corresponding file under
`$SKILL_DIR/profiles/` if the task is substantial or unfamiliar:

- `finance-ir.md`
- `product-platform.md`
- `gtm-growth.md`
- `engineering-platform.md`
- `consumer-retail.md`
- `template-and-edit.md`
- `appendix-heavy.md`

Create `$WORKSPACE/profile-plan.txt` with:

- task mode
- primary deck-profile
- secondary profile gates, if any
- required proof objects
- source/asset requirements
- brand authenticity constraints for logos, icons, mascots, screenshots, and
  other identity assets
- profile-specific QA gates
- known missing inputs

## Phase 0: Source And Reference Read

For every source or reference deck:

- Render it to PNGs or PDF pages.
- Make a contact sheet.
- Extract slide text.

Output:
Before building slides, create `$WORKSPACE/contact-sheet-plan.txt`.

For a 10-slide deck, use at least 5 distinct macro-layout families, such as:

- cover with metric rail
- editorial product map
- horizontal bar proof with margin notes
- donut or mix proof with ranked evidence table
- line chart with right-side KPI stack
- sequential bar chart with side summary rail
- two-series cash chart with margin callout
- roadmap timeline
- dense appendix table
- dark appendix/source page

Hard gates:

- no more than 2 card-grid slides in a 10-slide deck
- no 3 consecutive slides may share the same macro layout
- no repeated `title + subtitle + boxed panel grid` cadence
- no rounded card default unless the data relationship requires containment
- no decorative boxes around prose

The contact sheet must look authored before details are read.

## Phase 4: Editable Build

Build one ESM slide module per slide in `$SLIDES_DIR`, exporting numbered
functions such as:

```js
export async function slide01(presentation, ctx) {
  const slide = presentation.slides.add();
  // editable artifact-tool presentation JSX content
  return slide;
}
```

Prefer native editable shapes, lines, text, tables, and chart-like constructs.
For charts, native chart helpers are allowed, but authored editable chart
systems built from shapes are acceptable when they give better label placement
and visual polish.

### Structured Visual Precision Contract

Charts, diagrams, connectors, boxes, tables, and flows are high-risk proof
objects. Treat them as geometry systems, not decoration.

Before authoring any structured visual, define:

- what the visual must prove
- the primary reading order
- each node / mark / series and what it means
- each edge / connector and what relationship it encodes
- each container / box and what grouping or containment it means
- the intended alignment, spacing, and label attachment rules

Hard build rules:

- Connected series must be rendered as one continuous editable path or as a
  verified native chart series. Do not fake a line with separately rotated
  rectangles, disconnected strokes, floating slashes, or decorative arrows.
- Every connected series must pass through its intended markers and preserve
  the correct point order after render/export.
- In diagrams, connectors must visibly attach to the correct source and target,
  follow the intended direction, avoid unrelated objects, and terminate cleanly
  without ambiguous crossings.
- Do not use arrows unless directionality matters. If direction matters, arrow
  heads must be consistent, legible, and semantically meaningful.
- Boxes must imply a real grouping, comparison, lane, stage, or containment
  relationship. Remove containers that only decorate prose.

    rule(slide, ctx, xx, y - 8, 1, idx % 2 ? style.accent2 : style.accent, 58);
    text(slide, ctx, metric.value, xx + 14, y, 190, 34, { size: 26, color: style.ink, face: style.serif, bold: true, style });

  });
}

+    const value = argv[index + 1];
+    if (!value || value.startsWith("--")) {

+    } else {

[452] assistant: 基本可以：把 `Kid-Creator-0.1.0.dmg` 发到其他 Mac，双击打开，然后把 `Kid Creator.app` 拖到 `Applications` 就能安装。

但注意一点：现在这个包是 **ad-hoc 签名，没有 Apple Developer ID 公证**。所以其他 Mac 第一次打开时可能会被 Gatekeeper 拦截，提示“无法验证开发者”。

解决方式：

1. 右键 `Kid Creator.app`
2. 选择 `打开`
3. 再点一次 `打开`

这样通常可以运行。

如果你要正式分发给很多用户，就需要：
- Apple Developer ID 签名
- Hardened Runtime
- Notarization 公证
- stapler 票据附加

自己测试或发给熟悉的人，直接发 DMG 就可以。

import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import fsSync from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

export const DEFAULT_SLIDE_SIZE = { width: 1280, height: 720 };
const MIN_ARTIFACT_TOOL_VERSION = "2.7.3";

export function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = {};
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
    const key = argv[index];
    if (!key.startsWith("--")) {
      throw new Error(`Unexpected positional argument: ${key}`);
    }
    const value = argv[index + 1];
    if (!value || value.startsWith("--")) {
      args[key.slice(2)] = true;
      continue;
    }
    args[key.slice(2)] = value;
    index += 1;
  }
  return args;
}

export function requireArg(args, key) {
  const value = args[key];
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(`Missing required --${key}`);
  }
  return value;
}

export function parseSlideSize(value) {
  if (!value) return DEFAULT_SLIDE_SIZE;
  const match = String(value).match(/^(\d+)x(\d+)$/);
  if (!match) {
    throw new Error(`Expected slide size as WIDTHxHEIGHT, got: ${value}`);
  }
  return {
    width: Number.parseInt(match[1], 10),
    height: Number.parseInt(match[2], 10),
  };
}

export function padSlideNumber(value) {
  return String(value).padStart(2, "0");
}

export function slideNumberFromModuleName(filePath) {
  const base = path.basename(filePath);
  const match = base.match(/^slide[-_]?(\d+)\.mjs$/i);
  if (!match) return undefined;
  return Number.parseInt(match[1], 10);
}

function defaultRuntimeNodeModules() {
  return path.join(
    process.env.HOME || process.cwd(),
    ".cache",
    "codex-runtimes",
    ["codex", "primary", "runtime"].join("-"),
    "dependencies",
    "node",
    "node_modules",
  );
}

function packageJsonPath(packageDir) {
  return path.joi<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="781" />T_TOOL_VERSION} or newer.`,
        `Checked ${packageDir}; found @oai/artifact-tool@${packageJson.version || "unknown"}.`,
      ].join("\n"),
    );
  }
  const entrypoint = artifactToolEntrypointPath(packageDir);
  if (!entrypoint) {
    throw new Error(
      [
        `Expected ${context} to include a built artifact-tool entrypoint.`,
        `Checked ${path.join(packageDir, "dist", "node", "artifact_tool.mjs")} and ${path.join(packageDir, "dist", "artifact_tool.mjs")}.`,
        "Build the local artifact-tool bundle before retrying.",
      ].join("\n"),
    );
  }
  validateArtifactToolPresentationJsx(packageDir, context);
  return { packageDir: path.resolve(packageDir), entrypoint: path.resolve(entrypoint) };
}

function findArtifactToolPackage() {
  return validateArtifactToolPackage(
    runtimePackagePath("@oai/artifact-tool"),
    "the bundled Codex runtime @oai/artifact-tool package",
  );
}

function runtimePackagePath(packageName) {
  return path.join(defaultRuntimeNodeModules(), ...packageName.split("/"));
}

function findOptionalRuntimePackage(packageName) {
  const runtimePackage = runtimePackagePath(packageName);
  if (isNamedPackage(runtimePackage, packageName)) {
    return runtimePackage;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName) {
  return path.join(workspaceDir, "node_modules", ...packageName.split("/"));
}

async function ensureWorkspacePackage(workspaceDir, packageName, sourcePackage) {
  const target = workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName);
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });

  const existing = await fs.lstat(target).catch(() => undefined);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      fsSync.existsSync(packageJsonPath(target)) &&
      isNamedPackage(target, packageName) &&
      isSamePackageLocation(target, sourcePackage)
    ) {
      return target;
    }
    if (existing.isSymbolicLink()) {
      await fs.rm(target, { recursive: true, force: true });

---
name: Presentations
description: Build premium editorial analytics PPTX decks with artifact-tool presentation JSX, using ruthless narrative editing, chart-first storytelling, rendered critique, and iteration until the output beats the reference deck.
---

# Presentations

`Presentations` is for serious, high-polish presentation work where "clean" is
not enough. The target is an editable PowerPoint deck that feels like a strong
editor, a strong analyst, and a strong designer built it together.

Use this skill for analytics narratives, investor/operating reviews, strategy
stories, product/business performance decks, and any PPTX task where the user
asks to beat a reference deck.

## Operating Contract

Use artifact-tool presentation JSX only. The bundled Codex runtime provides
`@oai/artifact-tool` version 2.7.3 or newer and exposes
`@oai/artifact-tool/presentation-jsx`.

Do not require or import a separate presentation runtime package. PPTX
export/render behavior must be accessed through artifact-tool.

For generated raster images or reference comps, use the Codex imagegen tool.
Bundled scripts may write imagegen prompt files, but must not call external
image APIs or read API keys.

Thread-scoped paths:

- `SKILL_DIR=<absolute path to the installed Presentations skill>`
- `THREAD_ID=${CODEX_THREAD_ID:-manual-<timestamp-or-short-random-suffix>}`
- `WORKSPACE=$PWD/outputs/$THREAD_ID/presentations/<task-slug>`
- `SLIDES_DIR=$WORKSPACE/slides`
- `PREVIEW_DIR=$WORKSPACE/preview`
- `LAYOUT_DIR=$WORKSPACE/layout`
- `ASSET_DIR=$WORKSPACE/assets`
- `QA_DIR=$WORKSPACE/qa`
- `OUTPUT_DIR=<user-provided output dir if any, otherwise $WORKSPACE/output>`
- `FINAL_PPTX=$OUTPUT_DIR/<relevant-deck-title-slug>.pptx`

Use absolute paths in commands and handoffs. Keep all generated planning text
notes, preview PNGs, contact sheets, layout JSON, imagegen prompt f<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1093" />ect. Requires real assets or explicit asset
  provenance, image quality, editorial hierarchy, and client-ready copy.
- `template-following`: user supplied a template/source deck whose slides must
  be treated as the editable starting point. Requires `template-audit.txt`,
  `template-frame-map.json`, and `deviation-log.txt`; follow
  `template-and-edit.md` for the exact duplicate-first starter method.
- `targeted-edit-data`: add or edit a data/comparison slide. Requires exact
  calculations before visual work and a native-looking insertion into the
  existing deck.
- `targeted-edit-media`: add headshots, logos, screenshots, or other media.
  Requires identity/source verification, consistent crops, and preservation of
  the source deck layout grammar.
- `appendix-heavy`: dense appendix, tables, disclosures, or source packs.
  Requires index/page markers, readable small-type thresholds, table grammar,
  and explicit source-density rules.

If more than one profile applies, pick the profile that creates the highest
delivery risk as primary, then list secondary gates in the claim spine. Example:
a finance investor deck with a supplied template is `finance-ir` primary and
`template-following` secondary.

When a profile is selected, read its corresponding file under
`$SKILL_DIR/profiles/` if the task is substantial or unfamiliar:

- `finance-ir.md`
- `product-platform.md`
- `gtm-growth.md`
- `engineering-platform.md`
- `consumer-retail.md`
- `template-and-edit.md`
- `appendix-heavy.md`

Create `$WORKSPACE/profile-plan.txt` with:

- task mode
- primary deck-profile
- secondary profile gates, if any
- required proof objects
- source/asset requirements
- brand authenticity constraints for logos, icons, mascots, screenshots, and
  other identity assets
- profile-specific QA gates
- known missing inputs

## Phase 0: Source And Reference Read

For every source or reference deck:

- Render it to PNGs or PDF pages.
- Make a contact sheet.
- Extract slide text.

Output:
Before building slides, create `$WORKSPACE/contact-sheet-plan.txt`.

For a 10-slide deck, use at least 5 distinct macro-layout families, such as:

- cover with metric rail
- editorial product map
- horizontal bar proof with margin notes
- donut or mix proof with ranked evidence table
- line chart with right-side KPI stack
- sequential bar chart with side summary rail
- two-series cash chart with margin callout
- roadmap timeline
- dense appendix table
- dark appendix/source page

Hard gates:

- no more than 2 card-grid slides in a 10-slide deck
- no 3 consecutive slides may share the same macro layout
- no repeated `title + subtitle + boxed panel grid` cadence
- no rounded card default unless the data relationship requires containment
- no decorative boxes around prose

The contact sheet must look authored before details are read.

## Phase 4: Editable Build

Build one ESM slide module per slide in `$SLIDES_DIR`, exporting numbered
functions such as:

```js
export async function slide01(presentation, ctx) {
  const slide = presentation.slides.add();
  // editable artifact-tool presentation JSX content
  return slide;
}
```

Prefer native editable shapes, lines, text, tables, and chart-like constructs.
For charts, native chart helpers are allowed, but authored editable chart
systems built from shapes are acceptable when they give better label placement
and visual polish.

### Structured Visual Precision Contract

Charts, diagrams, connectors, boxes, tables, and flows are high-risk proof
objects. Treat them as geometry systems, not decoration.

Before authoring any structured visual, define:

- what the visual must prove
- the primary reading order
- each node / mark / series and what it means
- each edge / connector and what relationship it encodes
- each container / box and what grouping or containment it means
- the intended alignment, spacing, and label attachment rules

Hard build rules:

- Connected series must be rendered as one continuous editable path or as a
  verified native chart series. Do not fake a line with separately rotated
  rectangles, disconnected strokes, floating slashes, or decorative arrows.
- Every connected series must pass through its intended markers and preserve
  the correct point order after render/export.
- In diagrams, connectors must visibly attach to the correct source and target,
  follow the intended direction, avoid unrelated objects, and terminate cleanly
  without ambiguous crossings.
- Do not use arrows unless directionality matters. If direction matters, arrow
  heads must be consistent, legible, and semantically meaningful.
- Boxes must imply a real grouping, comparison, lane, stage, or containment
  relationship. Remove containers that only decorate prose.

function metricRail(slide, ctx, style, x, y, metrics) {
  metrics.forEach((metric, idx) => {
    const xx = x + idx * 240;
    rule(slide, ctx, xx, y - 8, 1, idx % 2 ? style.accent2 : style.accent, 58);
    text(slide, ctx, metric.value, xx + 14, y, 190, 34, { size: 26, color: style.ink, face: style.serif, bold: true, style });
    text(slide, ctx, metric.label, xx + 14, y + 40, 190, 18, { size: 9.5, color: style.soft, bold: true, style });
    text(slide, ctx, metric.note, xx + 14, y + 58, 190, 20, { size: 8.5, color: style.soft, style });
  });
}

function slideCover(presentation, ctx, row, meta) {

import Foundation

@MainActor
final class AppStore: ObservableObject {
    @Published var selectedMode: CreatorMode = .chat {
        didSet { messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode) }
    }
    @Published var messages: [ChatMessage] = []
    @Published var draft = ""
    @Published var apiKey = ""
    @Published var selectedProvider: ModelProvider = .deepseek {
        didSet {
            UserDefaults.standard.set(selectedProvider.rawValue, forKey: "selectedProvider")
            loadProviderSettings()
        }
    }
    @Published var model = ModelProvider.deepseek.defaultModel
    @Published var apiBaseURL = ModelProvider.deepseek.defaultBaseURL.absoluteString
    @Published var statusText = "请输入 DeepSeek API Key"
    @Published var isSending = false
    @Published var attachedFile: AttachedFile?
    @Published var lastError: String?
    @Published var exportedArtifacts: [WorkArtifact] = []
    @Published var exportStatus: String?

    private let client = DeepSeekClient()
    private let keychain = KeychainStore()
    private let conversationStore = ConversationStore()
    private let artifactExporter = ArtifactExporter()

    var canSend: Bool {
        !apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !draft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !isSending
    }

    var latestAssistantMessage: ChatMessage? {
        messages.last { $0.role == .assistant }
    }

    var canExportLatest: Bool {
        latestAssistantMessage != nil
    }

    init() {
        if let savedProvider = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "selectedProvider"),
           let provider = ModelProvider(rawValue: savedProvider) {
            selectedProvider = provider
        }
        loadProviderSettings()
        messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode)
    }

    func s<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />tant safety rules: The user may be a child. Keep content age-appropriate, educational, and encouraging. Do not ask for private personal information. For local files, explain before changing. Do not claim actions were executed outside this chat.
        """
        return [
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: selectedMode.systemPrompt),
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: safety)
        ] + recentMessages
    }

    private func buildUserContent(prompt: String) -> String {
        guard let attachedFile else { return prompt }

        let truncation = attachedFile.wasTruncated ? "\n\n注意：文件内容太长，下面只包含前一部分。" : ""
        return """
        \(prompt)

        附件文件：\(attachedFile.name)
        路径：\(attachedFile.path)\(truncation)

        ```text
        \(attachedFile.content)
        ```
        """
    }

    private func loadProviderSettings() {
        apiKey = (try? keychain.load(provider: selectedProvider)) ?? ""
        model = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: modelDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultModel
        apiBaseURL = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL.absoluteString
        statusText = apiKey.isEmpty ? "请输入 \(selectedProvider.displayName) API Key" : "\(selectedProvider.displayName) Key 已保存"
    }

    private func modelDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "model.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func endpointDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "endpoint.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func effectiveBaseURL() -> URL {
        let trimmed = apiBaseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        UserDefaults.standard.set(trimmed, forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider))
        return URL(string: trimmed) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct MessageBubbleView: View {
    let message: ChatMessage

    var body: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .top) {
            if message.role == .user { Spacer(minLength: 80) }

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {

import Foundation

@MainActor
final class AppStore: ObservableObject {
    @Published var selectedMode: CreatorMode = .chat {
        didSet { messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode) }
    }
    @Published var messages: [ChatMessage] = []
    @Published var draft = ""
    @Published var apiKey = ""
    @Published var selectedProvider: ModelProvider = .deepseek {
        didSet {
            UserDefaults.standard.set(selectedProvider.rawValue, forKey: "selectedProvider")
            loadProviderSettings()
        }
    }
    @Published var model = ModelProvider.deepseek.defaultModel
    @Published var apiBaseURL = ModelProvider.deepseek.defaultBaseURL.absoluteString
    @Published var statusText = "请输入 DeepSeek API Key"
    @Published var isSending = false
    @Published var attachedFile: AttachedFile?
    @Published var lastError: String?
    @Published var exportedArtifacts: [WorkArtifact] = []
    @Published var exportStatus: String?

    private let client = DeepSeekClient()
    private let keychain = KeychainStore()
    private let conversationStore = ConversationStore()
    private let artifactExporter = ArtifactExporter()

    var canSend: Bool {
        !apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !draft.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty &&
        !isSending
    }

    var latestAssistantMessage: ChatMessage? {
        messages.last { $0.role == .assistant }
    }

    var canExportLatest: Bool {
        latestAssistantMessage != nil
    }

    init() {
        if let savedProvider = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "selectedProvider"),
           let provider = ModelProvider(rawValue: savedProvider) {
            selectedProvider = provider
        }
        loadProviderSettings()
        messages = conversationStore.load(mode: selectedMode)
    }

    func s<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1126" />tant safety rules: The user may be a child. Keep content age-appropriate, educational, and encouraging. Do not ask for private personal information. For local files, explain before changing. Do not claim actions were executed outside this chat.
        """
        return [
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: selectedMode.systemPrompt),
            ChatMessage(role: .system, content: safety)
        ] + recentMessages
    }

    private func buildUserContent(prompt: String) -> String {
        guard let attachedFile else { return prompt }

        let truncation = attachedFile.wasTruncated ? "\n\n注意：文件内容太长，下面只包含前一部分。" : ""
        return """
        \(prompt)

        附件文件：\(attachedFile.name)
        路径：\(attachedFile.path)\(truncation)

        ```text
        \(attachedFile.content)
        ```
        """
    }

    private func loadProviderSettings() {
        apiKey = (try? keychain.load(provider: selectedProvider)) ?? ""
        model = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: modelDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultModel
        apiBaseURL = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL.absoluteString
        statusText = apiKey.isEmpty ? "请输入 \(selectedProvider.displayName) API Key" : "\(selectedProvider.displayName) Key 已保存"
    }

    private func modelDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "model.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func endpointDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "endpoint.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func effectiveBaseURL() -> URL {
        let trimmed = apiBaseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        UserDefaults.standard.set(trimmed, forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider))
        return URL(string: trimmed) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL
    }
}

import Foundation

struct PPTSkillRunner {
    private let skillDir = "/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium"

    func exportPPTX(content: String, outputDirectory: URL) throws -> URL {
        let contentURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation-source.txt")
        try content.write(to: contentURL, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)

        let scriptURL = try runnerScriptURL()
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/env")
        process.arguments = [
            "node",
            scriptURL.path,
            "--content", contentURL.path,
            "--output-dir", outputDirectory.path,
            "--skill-dir", skillDir
        ]

        let outputPipe = Pipe()
        let errorPipe = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = outputPipe
        process.standardError = errorPipe

        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()

        let stdout = String(data: outputPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        let stderr = String(data: errorPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""

        guard process.terminationStatus == 0 else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.failed(stdout: stdout, stderr: stderr)
        }

        let pptxURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation.pptx")
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: pptxURL.path) else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingOutput
        }
        return pptxURL
    }

    private func runnerScriptURL() throws -> URL {
        let bundleURL = Bundle.main.resourceURL?.appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if let bundleURL, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: bundleURL.path) {
            return bundleURL
        }

        let sourceURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: #filePath)
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: sourceURL.path) {
            return sourceURL
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingRunner
    }
}

enum PPTSkillRunnerError: LocalizedError {
    case missingRunner

        }
    }
}

import Foundation

struct PPTSkillRunner {
    private let skillDir = "/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium"

    func exportPPTX(content: String, outputDirectory: URL) throws -> URL {
        let contentURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation-source.txt")
        try content.write(to: contentURL, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)

        let scriptURL = try runnerScriptURL()
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/usr/bin/env")
        process.arguments = [
            "node",
            scriptURL.path,
            "--content", contentURL.path,
            "--output-dir", outputDirectory.path,
            "--skill-dir", skillDir
        ]

        let outputPipe = Pipe()
        let errorPipe = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = outputPipe
        process.standardError = errorPipe

        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()

        let stdout = String(data: outputPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        let stderr = String(data: errorPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""

        guard process.terminationStatus == 0 else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.failed(stdout: stdout, stderr: stderr)
        }

        let pptxURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation.pptx")
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: pptxURL.path) else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingOutput
        }
        return pptxURL
    }

    private func runnerScriptURL() throws -> URL {
        let bundleURL = Bundle.main.resourceURL?.appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if let bundleURL, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: bundleURL.path) {
            return bundleURL
        }

        let sourceURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: #filePath)
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: sourceURL.path) {
            return sourceURL
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingRunner
    }
}

enum PPTSkillRunnerError: LocalizedError {
    case missingRunner
    case missingOutput
    case failed(stdout: String, stderr: String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .missingRunner:
            return "找不到 PPT skill runner 脚本。"
        case .missingOutput:
            return "PPT skill 没有生成 presentation.pptx。"
        case .failed(let stdout, let stderr):
            return "PPT skill 导出失败。\n\(stdout)\n\(stderr)"
        }
    }
}

import Foundation

struct PPTSkillRunner {
    private let skillDir = "/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium"

    func exportPPTX(content: String, outputDirectory: URL) throws -> URL {
        let contentURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation-source.txt")
        try content.write(to: contentURL, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)

        let scriptURL = try runnerScriptURL()
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = try nodeURL()
        process.arguments = [
            scriptURL.path,
            "--content", contentURL.path,
            "--output-dir", outputDirectory.path,
            "--skill-dir", skillDir
        ]

        let outputPipe = Pipe()
        let errorPipe = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = outputPipe
        process.standardError = errorPipe

        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()

        let stdout = String(data: outputPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        let stderr = String(data: errorPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""

        guard process.terminationStatus == 0 else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.failed(stdout: stdout, stderr: stderr)
        }

        let pptxURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation.pptx")
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: pptxURL.path) else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingOutput
        }
        return pptxURL
    }

    private func runnerScriptURL() throws -> URL {
        let bundleURL = Bundle.main.resourceURL?.appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if let bundleURL, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: bundleURL.path) {
            return bundleURL
        }

        let sourceURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: #filePath)
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: sourceURL.path) {
            return sourceURL
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingRunner
    }

    private func nodeURL() throws -> URL {
        let candidates = [
            "/Users/vincet/.openclaw/tools/node-v22.22.0/bin/node",
            "/opt/homebrew/bin/node",
            "/usr/local/bin/node",
            "/usr/bin/node"
        ]

        for candidate in candidates where FileManager.default.isExecutableFile(atPath: candidate) {
            return URL(fileURLWithPath: candidate)
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingNode
    }
}

enum PPTSkillRunnerError: LocalizedError {
    case missingRunner
    case missingNode
    case missingOutput
    case failed(stdout: String, stderr: String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .missingRunner:
            return "找不到 PPT skill runner 脚本。"
        case .missingNode:
            return "找不到 Node.js，无法调用本地 PPT skill。"
        case .missingOutput:
            return "PPT skill 没有生成 presentation.pptx。"
        case .failed(let stdout, let stderr):
            return "PPT skill 导出失败。\n\(stdout)\n\(stderr)"
        }
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
DIST="$ROOT/dist"
BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"

cd "$ROOT"

pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

swift build

rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources"
cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"
cp "$ROOT/Assets/AppIcon.icns" "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/AppIcon.icns"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/SkillRunners"
cp "$ROOT/SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs" "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs"

cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>$APP_NAME</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>local.kidcreator.mac</string>
  <key>CFBundleIconFile</key>
  <string>AppIcon</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>Kid Creator</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>0.1.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>1</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>13.0</string>
  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
  <string>NSApplication</string>
</dict>
</plist>
PLIST

codesign --force --deep --sign - "$BUNDLE"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
  sleep 2
  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
else
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
fi

import Foundation

struct PPTSkillRunner {
    private let skillDir = "/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium"

    func exportPPTX(content: String, outputDirectory: URL) throws -> URL {
        let contentURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation-source.txt")
        try content.write(to: contentURL, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)

        let scriptURL = try runnerScriptURL()
        let process = Process()
        process.executableURL = try nodeURL()
        process.arguments = [
            scriptURL.path,
            "--content", contentURL.path,
            "--output-dir", outputDirectory.path,
            "--skill-dir", skillDir
        ]

        let outputPipe = Pipe()
        let errorPipe = Pipe()
        process.standardOutput = outputPipe
        process.standardError = errorPipe

        try process.run()
        process.waitUntilExit()

        let stdout = String(data: outputPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
        let stderr = String(data: errorPipe.fileHandleForReading.readDataToEndOfFile(), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""

        guard process.terminationStatus == 0 else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.failed(stdout: stdout, stderr: stderr)
        }

        let pptxURL = outputDirectory.appendingPathComponent("presentation.pptx")
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: pptxURL.path) else {
            throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingOutput
        }
        return pptxURL
    }

    private func runnerScriptURL() throws -> URL {
        let bundleURL = Bundle.main.resourceURL?.appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if let bundleURL, FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: bundleURL.path) {
            return bundleURL
        }

        let sourceURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: #filePath)
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .deletingLastPathComponent()
            .appendingPathComponent("SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs")
        if FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: sourceURL.path) {
            return sourceURL
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingRunner
    }

    private func nodeURL() throws -> URL {
        let candidates = [
            "/Users/vincet/.openclaw/tools/node-v22.22.0/bin/node",
            "/opt/homebrew/bin/node",
            "/usr/local/bin/node",
            "/usr/bin/node"
        ]

        for candidate in candidates where FileManager.default.isExecutableFile(atPath: candidate) {
            return URL(fileURLWithPath: candidate)
        }

        throw PPTSkillRunnerError.missingNode
    }
}

enum PPTSkillRunnerError: LocalizedError {
    case missingRunner
    case missingNode
    case missingOutput
    case failed(stdout: String, stderr: String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .missingRunner:
            return "找不到 PPT skill runner 脚本。"
        case .missingNode:
            return "找不到 Node.js，无法调用本地 PPT skill。"
        case .missingOutput:
            return "PPT skill 没有生成 presentation.pptx。"
        case .failed(let stdout, let stderr):
            return "PPT skill 导出失败。\n\(stdout)\n\(stderr)"
        }
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
APP_NAME="KidCreatorMac"
PRODUCT="$APP_NAME"
DIST="$ROOT/dist"
BUNDLE="$DIST/$APP_NAME.app"
EXECUTABLE="$ROOT/.build/debug/$PRODUCT"

cd "$ROOT"

pkill -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true

swift build

rm -rf "$BUNDLE"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources"
cp "$EXECUTABLE" "$BUNDLE/Contents/MacOS/$APP_NAME"
cp "$ROOT/Assets/AppIcon.icns" "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/AppIcon.icns"
mkdir -p "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/SkillRunners"
cp "$ROOT/SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs" "$BUNDLE/Contents/Resources/SkillRunners/ppt_skill_runner.mjs"

cat > "$BUNDLE/Contents/Info.plist" <<PLIST
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<dict>
  <key>CFBundleExecutable</key>
  <string>$APP_NAME</string>
  <key>CFBundleIdentifier</key>
  <string>local.kidcreator.mac</string>
  <key>CFBundleIconFile</key>
  <string>AppIcon</string>
  <key>CFBundleName</key>
  <string>Kid Creator</string>
  <key>CFBundlePackageType</key>
  <string>APPL</string>
  <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key>
  <string>0.1.0</string>
  <key>CFBundleVersion</key>
  <string>1</string>
  <key>LSMinimumSystemVersion</key>
  <string>13.0</string>
  <key>NSPrincipalClass</key>
  <string>NSApplication</string>
</dict>
</plist>
PLIST

codesign --force --deep --sign - "$BUNDLE"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "--no-open" ]]; then
  echo "Built $BUNDLE"
elif [[ "${1:-}" == "--verify" ]]; then
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
  sleep 2
  pgrep -x "$APP_NAME" >/dev/null
  echo "Launched $APP_NAME"
else
  /usr/bin/open -n "$BUNDLE"
fi

新的安装包反馈这个错误，分析


[662] assistant: 这个新错误说明：**新版 App 已经正确内置了 `presentations-premium` skill，但没有内置它依赖的 `@oai/artifact-tool` runtime。**

关键证据：

```text
Kid Creator.app/Contents/Resources/Skills/presentations-premium/scripts/build_artifact_deck.mjs
```

这说明现在确实在跑 App 内置的 skill 文件，不再是旧的 `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/...` 路径。

失败点是：

```text
Checked /Users/fm/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/@oai/artifact-tool; found missing package.json.
```

也就是说 `presentations-premium` 这个 skill 不是独立的，它依赖 Codex 本机 runtime 里的：

```text
@oai/artifact-tool
```

在我这台机器上有这个 runtime，所以能生成 PPT；另一台 Mac 用户 `fm` 没有 Codex runtime 缓存，所以缺依赖。

**结论**
现在缺的不是 skill 文件，而是 `presentations-premium` 的运行时依赖：

```text
@oai/artifact-tool
```

**解决方向**
有两个选择：

1. **继续使用 `presentations-premium`**
   - 需要把 `@oai/artifact-tool` runtime 也打包进 DMG
   - 同时修改 `artifact_tool_utils.mjs` 或环境变量，让它从 App bundle 里找 runtime
   - 这是质量更高的路线，但包会变大，改动也更多

2. **做一个 App 内置的轻量 PPTX 生成器**
   - 不依赖 Codex runtime
   - 直接在 App 里生成基础 `.pptx`
   - 跨 Mac 更稳定
   - 但视觉能力不如 `presentations-premium`

当前错误证明：只内置 `presentations-premium` 不够，必须一起内置 `@oai/artifact-tool`，否则别的 Mac 无法运行。

[663] user: deepseek的 api 能够使用@oai/artifact-tool吗。


[664] assistant: 不能直接使用。

`DeepSeek API` 只负责生成文本/代码内容，比如：
- PPT 大纲
- 每页标题
- 每页要点
- JSON deck spec
- slide module 代码

但 `@oai/artifact-tool` 是本地 Node 运行时里的一个包，用来把这些“代码化的 PPT 页面”真正渲染、导出成 `.pptx`。它不是模型 API，也不是 DeepSeek 提供的能力。

所以链路应该是：

```text
DeepSeek API
  -> 生成 PPT 内容 / deck spec / slide 代码

本地 @oai/artifact-tool
  -> 读取 slide 代码
  -> 渲染页面
  -> 导出 presentation.pptx
```

你现在另一台电脑报错，是因为：

```text
DeepSeek 已经能生成内容
但本地缺 @oai/artifact-tool
所以无法导出 PPTX
```

如果要让 DMG 在任意 Mac 上可用，需要二选一：

1. 把 `@oai/artifact-tool` 和它依赖的 runtime 一起内置进 App。
2. 放弃 `@oai/artifact-tool`，改用 App 自带的轻量 PPTX 生成器。

DeepSeek 不能替代 `@oai/artifact-tool`，它只能帮我们生成可交给工具执行的内容。

function findArtifactToolPackage() {
  return validateArtifactToolPackage(
    runtimePackagePath("@oai/artifact-tool"),
    "the bundled Codex runtime @oai/artifact-tool package",
  );
}

function runtimePackagePath(packageName) {
  return path.join(defaultRuntimeNodeModules(), ...packageName.split("/"));
}

function findOptionalRuntimePackage(packageName) {
  const runtimePackage = runtimePackagePath(packageName);
  if (isNamedPackage(runtimePackage, packageName)) {
    return runtimePackage;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName) {
  return path.join(workspaceDir, "node_modules", ...packageName.split("/"));
}

async function ensureWorkspacePackage(workspaceDir, packageName, sourcePackage) {
  const target = workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName);
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });

  const existing = await fs.lstat(target).catch(() => undefined);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      fsSync.existsSync(packageJsonPath(target)) &&
      isNamedPackage(target, packageName) &&
      isSamePackageLocation(target, sourcePackage)
    ) {
      return target;
    }
    if (existing.isSymbolicLink()) {
      await fs.rm(target, { recursive: true, force: true });
    } else {
      throw new Error(`${target} exists but is not ${packageName}.`);
    }
  }

  await fs.symlink(sourcePackage, target, process.platform === "win32" ? "junction" : "dir");
  return target;
}

async function ensureModulePackage(workspaceDir) {
  const packagePath = path.join(workspaceDir, "package.json");
  if (!fsSync.existsSync(packagePath)) {
    await fs.writeFile(
      packagePath,
      `${JSON.stringify({ private: true, type: "module" }, null, 2)}\n`,
      "utf8",
    );
    return;
  }

  const packageJson = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(packagePath, "utf8"));
  if (packageJson.type !== "module") {
    throw new Error(`${packagePath} exists but does not set "type": "module".`);
  }
}

export async function ensureArtifactToolWorkspace(workspaceDir) {
  const resolvedWorkspace = path.resolve(workspaceDir);
  await fs.mkdir(resolvedWorkspace, { recursive: true });
  await ensureModulePackage(resolvedWorkspace);

  const { packageDir: sourcePackage } = findArtifactToolPackage();
  await ensureWorkspacePackage(resolvedWorkspace, "@oai/artifact-tool", sourcePackage);

  const lucidePackage = findOptionalRuntimePackage("lucide");
  if (lucidePackage) {
    await ensureWorkspacePackage(resolvedWorkspace, "lucide", lucidePackage);
  }

  return { workspaceDir: resolvedWorkspace, packageDir: sourcePackage };
}

export async function importArtifactTool(workspaceDir) {
  const { entrypoint } = findArtifactToolPackage();
  return import(pathToFileURL(entrypoint).href);
}

export async function importModuleFresh(modulePath) {
  const resolved = path.resolve(modulePath);
  const stat = await fs.stat(resolved);
  return import(`${pathToFileURL(resolved).href}?mtime=${stat.mtimeMs}`);
}

[662] assistant: 这个新错误说明：**新版 App 已经正确内置了 `presentations-premium` skill，但没有内置它依赖的 `@oai/artifact-tool` runtime。**

关键证据：

```text
Kid Creator.app/Contents/Resources/Skills/presentations-premium/scripts/build_artifact_deck.mjs
```

这说明现在确实在跑 App 内置的 skill 文件，不再是旧的 `/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/...` 路径。

失败点是：

```text
Checked /Users/fm/.cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/node_modules/@oai/artifact-tool; found missing package.json.
```

也就是说 `presentations-premium` 这个 skill 不是独立的，它依赖 Codex 本机 runtime 里的：

```text
@oai/artifact-tool
```

在我这台机器上有这个 runtime，所以能生成 PPT；另一台 Mac 用户 `fm` 没有 Codex runtime 缓存，所以缺依赖。

**结论**
现在缺的不是 skill 文件，而是 `presentations-premium` 的运行时依赖：

```text
@oai/artifact-tool
```

**解决方向**
有两个选择：

1. **继续使用 `presentations-premium`**
   - 需要把 `@oai/artifact-tool` runtime 也打包进 DMG
   - 同时修改 `artifact_tool_utils.mjs` 或环境变量，让它从 App bundle 里找 runtime
   - 这是质量更高的路线，但包会变大，改动也更多

2. **做一个 App 内置的轻量 PPTX 生成器**
   - 不依赖 Codex runtime
   - 直接在 App 里生成基础 `.pptx`
   - 跨 Mac 更稳定
   - 但视觉能力不如 `presentations-premium`

当前错误证明：只内置 `presentations-premium` 不够，必须一起内置 `@oai/artifact-tool`，否则别的 Mac 无法运行。

[663] user: deepseek的 api 能够使用@oai/artifact-tool吗。


[664] assistant: 不能直接使用。

`DeepSeek API` 只负责生成文本/代码内容，比如：
- PPT 大纲
- 每页标题
- 每页要点
- JSON deck spec
- slide module 代码

但 `@oai/artifact-tool` 是本地 Node 运行时里的一个包，用来把这些“代码化的 PPT 页面”真正渲染、导出成 `.pptx`。它不是模型 API，也不是 DeepSeek 提供的能力。

所以链路应该是：

```text
DeepSeek API
  -> 生成 PPT 内容 / deck spec / slide 代码

本地 @oai/artifact-tool
  -> 读取 slide 代码
  -> 渲染页面
  -> 导出 presentation.pptx
```

你现在另一台电脑报错，是因为：

```text
DeepSeek 已经能生成内容
但本地缺 @oai/artifact-tool
所以无法导出 PPTX
```

如果要让 DMG 在任意 Mac 上可用，需要二选一：

1. 把 `@oai/artifact-tool` 和它依赖的 runtime 一起内置进 App。
2. 放弃 `@oai/artifact-tool`，改用 App 自带的轻量 PPTX 生成器。

DeepSeek 不能替代 `@oai/artifact-tool`，它只能帮我们生成可交给工具执行的内容。

  validateArtifactToolPresentationJsx(packageDir, context);

function findArtifactToolPackage() {
  return validateArtifactToolPackage(
    runtimePackagePath("@oai/artifact-tool"),
    "the bundled Codex runtime @oai/artifact-tool package",
  );
}

function runtimePackagePath(packageName) {
  return path.join(defaultRuntimeNodeModules(), ...packageName.split("/"));
}

function findOptionalRuntimePackage(packageName) {
  const runtimePackage = runtimePackagePath(packageName);
  if (isNamedPackage(runtimePackage, packageName)) {
    return runtimePackage;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName) {
  return path.join(workspaceDir, "node_modules", ...packageName.split("/"));
}

async function ensureWorkspacePackage(workspaceDir, packageName, sourcePackage) {
  const target = workspacePackagePath(workspaceDir, packageName);
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });

  const existing = await fs.lstat(target).catch(() => undefined);
  if (existing) {
    if (
      fsSync.existsSync(packageJsonPath(target)) &&
      isNamedPackage(target, packageName) &&
      isSamePackageLocation(target, sourcePackage)
    ) {
      return target;
    }
    if (existing.isSymbolicLink()) {
      await fs.rm(target, { recursive: true, force: true });
    } else {
      throw new Error(`${target} exists but is not ${packageName}.`);
    }
  }

  await fs.symlink(sourcePackage, target, process.platform === "win32" ? "junction" : "dir");
  return target;
}

async function ensureModulePackage(workspaceDir) {
  const packagePath = path.join(workspaceDir, "package.json");
  if (!fsSync.existsSync(packagePath)) {
    await fs.writeFile(
      packagePath,
      `${JSON.stringify({ private: true, type: "module" }, null, 2)}\n`,
      "utf8",
    );
    return;
  }

  const packageJson = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(packagePath, "utf8"));
  if (packageJson.type !== "module") {
    throw new Error(`${packagePath} exists but does not set "type": "module".`);
  }
}

export async function ensureArtifactToolWorkspace(workspaceDir) {
  const resolvedWorkspace = path.resolve(workspaceDir);
  await fs.mkdir(resolvedWorkspace, { recursive: true });
  await ensureModulePackage(resolvedWorkspace);

  const { packageDir: sourcePackage } = findArtifactToolPackage();
  await ensureWorkspacePackage(resolvedWorkspace, "@oai/artifact-tool", sourcePackage);

  const lucidePackage = findOptionalRuntimePackage("lucide");
  if (lucidePackage) {
    await ensureWorkspacePackage(resolvedWorkspace, "lucide", lucidePackage);
  }

  return { workspaceDir: resolvedWorkspace, packageDir: sourcePackage };
}

export async function importArtifactTool(workspaceDir) {
  const { entrypoint } = findArtifactToolPackage();
  return import(pathToFileURL(entrypoint).href);
}

export async function importModuleFresh(modulePath) {
  const resolved = path.resolve(modulePath);
  const stat = await fs.stat(resolved);
  return import(`${pathToFileURL(resolved).href}?mtime=${stat.mtimeMs}`);
}

export function resolveSlideFunction(module, exportName, slideNumber) {
  const candidates = [];
  if (exportName) candidates.push(exportName);
  if (slideNumber !== undefined) {
    candidates.push(`slide${padSlideNumber(slideNumber)}`, `slide${slideNumber}`);
  }
  candidates.push("addSlide", "default");

## presentation.pptx: /Users/vincet/Downloads/presentation.pptx

## My request for Codex:
已经可以生成ppt文件，但是deepseek无法生成合理的内容；如何实现调用deepseek回复后生成合理的ppt内容，文字讨论可行性


[736] assistant: 可行，但要把“DeepSeek 直接写 PPT”改成“两段式生成”，不要让它一次性自由发挥。

现在的问题大概率是：

```text
用户需求 -> DeepSeek 一次性回复 -> 直接转 PPT
```

这样 DeepSeek 容易输出：
- 结构散
- 页数不稳定
- 每页内容过长
- 没有页面层级
- 没有讲稿/视觉说明
- JSON 不标准
- PPT runner 只能被动排版，不能判断内容质量

更合理的产品链路应该是：

```text
用户输入主题
  -> DeepSeek 先生成 PPT 内容结构化 JSON
  -> App 校验 JSON
  -> 不合格则让 DeepSeek 自我修正
  -> App 把合格 JSON 转成 slide module
  -> artifact-tool 导出 PPTX
  -> 对话里返回 PPT 文件链接
```

**建议的 PPT 生成协议**
让 DeepSeek 固定只输出这个结构：

```json
{
  "title": "太阳系小课堂",
  "audience": "小学三年级",
  "style": "有趣、清楚、适合课堂展示",
  "slides": [
    {
      "layout": "cover",
      "title": "太阳系小课堂",
      "subtitle": "认识我们的宇宙邻居",
      "speakerNotes": "今天我们来认识太阳系。"
    },
    {
      "layout": "bullets",
      "title": "太阳系里有什么？",
      "bullets": [
        "太阳在中间，像一个大火球",
        "八大行星围着太阳转",
        "地球是我们生活的家"
      ],
      "visual": "太阳在中心，行星围绕太阳的简单示意图",
      "speakerNotes": "这一页介绍太阳系的基本组成。"
    }
  ]
}
```

**App 需要做校验**
DeepSeek 返回后不要马上生成 PPT，先检查：

- 是否是合法 JSON
- 是否有 `title`
- `slides` 是否存在
- 页数是否在 3-8 页之间
- 每页是否有 `title`
- 每页 bullets 是否不超过 5 条
- 每条 bullet 是否不超过 30-40 字
- 是否有 `speakerNotes`
- 是否包含儿童不适合内容
- 是否没有真实姓名、学校、电话等隐私字段

如果不合格，App 自动发第二次请求：

```text
你刚才的 JSON 不符合要求。
问题：
1. 第 2 页 bullets 太长
2. 第 4 页缺少 speakerNotes
请只返回修正后的 JSON，不要解释。
```

**推荐的 DeepSeek system prompt**
PPT 模式里可以单独使用更强约束：

```text
你是儿童教育 PPT 内容策划师。
你只输出严格 JSON，不输出 Markdown，不输出解释。
目标是生成适合小学生展示的 PPT 内容。

规则：
1. 每页只表达一个核心观点
2. 每页标题不超过 18 个中文字符
3. 每页 bullet 3-5 条
4. 每条 bullet 不超过 28 个中文字符
5. 必须包含 speakerNotes
6. 必须包含 visual
7. 内容适合 6-12 岁儿童
8. 不得收集真实姓名、学校、地址、电话
9. 不得输出代码块
10. 输出必须能被 JSON.parse 解析
```

**更稳的三段式**
如果要效果更好，可以三段：

```text
第一步：生成大纲
第二步：扩展成结构化 slide JSON
第三步：质量检查并修正
```

具体链路：

1. **Outline Pass**
   - 生成 5 页标题
   - 确定故事线
   - 不生成 PPT

2. **Slide Spec Pass**
   - 按固定 schema 输出 JSON
   - 每页带 layout、title、bullets、visual、speakerNotes

3. **Critic Pass**
   - 检查是否像儿童课堂 PPT
   - 检查是否过长、重复、抽象
   - 返回修正版 JSON

最后才交给本地 PPT runner。

**关键点**
DeepSeek 不应该直接“做 PPT 文件”。它只负责生成：

```text
结构化 PPT 内容
```

真正生成 PPT 的工作由：

```text
App + presentations-premium + artifact-tool
```

完成。

所以正确分工是：

| 角色 | 负责什么 |
|---|---|
| DeepSeek | 内容规划、结构化 JSON、讲稿 |
| App | 校验、重试、保存、链接展示 |
| PPT skill | 页面代码生成、布局、导出 PPTX |
| artifact-tool | 真正生成 PowerPoint 文件 |

**结论**
完全可行。下一步应该改 PPT 模块的生成流程，不是简单把 DeepSeek 回复转 PPT，而是让 DeepSeek 输出严格 slide spec，并在 App 里做 JSON 校验和自动修正。这样 PPT 内容质量会稳定很多。

@MainActor
final class AppStore: ObservableObject {

    }

    private func appendArtifactMessage(_ artifact: WorkArtifact, mode: CreatorMode) {
        guard mode == .slides || mode == .website else { return }

        let typeName = mode == .slides ? "PPT 文件" : "网页文件"
        let message = ChatMessage(

            你可以直接打开下面的文件链接。
            """,
            artifactURL: artifact.url,
            artifactTitle: artifact.url.lastPathComponent
        )
        messages.append(message)
        conversationStore.save(messages, mode: selectedMode)
    }

    private func loadProviderSettings() {
        apiKey = (try? keychain.load(provider: selectedProvider)) ?? ""
        model = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: modelDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultModel
        apiBaseURL = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider)) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL.absoluteString
        statusText = apiKey.isEmpty ? "请输入 \(selectedProvider.displayName) API Key" : "\(selectedProvider.displayName) Key 已保存"
    }

    private func modelDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "model.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func endpointDefaultsKey(for provider: ModelProvider) -> String {
        "endpoint.\(provider.rawValue)"
    }

    private func effectiveBaseURL() -> URL {
        let trimmed = apiBaseURL.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        UserDefaults.standard.set(trimmed, forKey: endpointDefaultsKey(for: selectedProvider))
        return URL(string: trimmed) ?? selectedProvider.defaultBaseURL
    }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node

import fs from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const args = {};
  for (let index = 0; index < argv.length; index += 1) {
    const key = argv[index];
    if (!key.startsWith("--")) throw new Error(`Unexpected argument: ${key}`);
    const value = argv[index + 1];
    if (!value || value.startsWith("--")) {
      args[key.slice(2)] = true;
    } else {
      args[key.slice(2)] = value;
      index += 1;
    }
  }
  return args;
}

function requireArg(args, key) {
  if (!args[key]) throw new Error(`Missing --${key}`);
  return args[key];
}

function escapeJS(value) {
  return JSON.stringify(String(value ?? ""));
}

function stripFence(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/^```[a-zA-Z0-9_-]*\s*/g, "")
    .replace(/```$/g, "")
    .trim();
}

function extractJSON(content) {
  const fenced = content.match(/```(?:json|deck_spec|deck-spec)\s*([\s\S]*?)```/i);
  const candidates = [];
  if (fenced) candidates.push(stripFence(fenced[1]));
  const firstBrace = content.indexOf("{");
  const lastBrace = content.lastIndexOf("}");
  if (firstBrace >= 0 && lastBrace > firstBrace) {
    candidates.push(content.slice(firstBrace, lastBrace + 1));
  }

  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(candidate);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed.slides)) return parsed;
    } catch {
      // Continue to fallback parsing.
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

function titleFromContent(content) {
  for (const line of content.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed.startsWith("# ")) return trimmed.slice(2).trim();
    if (trimmed.startsWith("标题：")) return trimmed.slice(3).trim();
    if (/^title:/i.test(trimmed)) return trimmed.replace(/^title:\s*/i, "").trim();
  }
  return "<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1359" /> index, specs.length), "utf8");

}

async function main() {
  const args = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  const contentPath = path.resolve(requireArg(args, "content"));
  const outputDir = path.resolve(requireArg(args, "output-dir"));
  const skillDir = path.resolve(args["skill-dir"] || "/Users/vincet/Documents/skills/presentations-premium");
  const content = await fs.readFile(contentPath, "utf8");
  const deck = normalizeDeck(content);

  const workspace = path.join(outputDir, "ppt-skill-workspace");
  const slidesDir = path.join(workspace, "slides");
  const previewDir = path.join(workspace, "preview");
  const layoutDir = path.join(workspace, "layout");
  const out = path.join(outputDir, "presentation.pptx");
  const manifest = path.join(outputDir, "ppt-skill-manifest.json");
  const specPath = path.join(outputDir, "deck-spec.json");
  await fs.mkdir(outputDir, { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(specPath, `${JSON.stringify(deck, null, 2)}\n`, "utf8");
  const slideCount = await writeSlides(deck, slidesDir);

  const builder = path.join(skillDir, "scripts", "build_artifact_deck.mjs");
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [
    builder,
    "--slides-dir", slidesDir,
    "--out", out,
    "--preview-dir", previewDir,
    "--layout-dir", layoutDir,
    "--manifest", manifest,
    "--slide-count", String(slideCount),
  ], {
    encoding: "utf8",
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      SKILL_DIR: skillDir,
      KID_CREATOR_NODE_MODULES: path.join(path.dirname(skillDir), "..", "NodeRuntime", "node_modules"),
    },
  });

  if (result.status !== 0) {
    throw new Error([
      "PPT skill export failed.",
      result.stdout.trim(),
      result.stderr.trim(),
    ].filter(Boolean).join("\n"));
  }

  console.log(JSON.stringify({ output: out, specPath, slideCount, manifest }, null, 2));
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error(error.stack || error.message || String(error));
  process.exit(1);
});

## My request for Codex:
